<div class="subch">General</div>


<span id="2c01" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.01 Application of Warning Signs

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.01 &middot; manual page 308 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.01/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The use of warning signs shall be based on an engineering study or on engineering judgment.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Warning signs shall be retroreflective or illuminated (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a21">Section 2A.21</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The use of warning signs should be kept to a minimum as the unnecessary use of warning signs tends to breed disrespect for all signs. In situations where the condition or activity is seasonal or temporary, the warning sign should be removed or covered when the condition or activity does not exist.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c02" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.02 Design of Warning Signs

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.02 &middot; manual page 308 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.02/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Except as provided in Paragraph 2 of this Section or unless specifically designated otherwise, all warning signs shall be diamond-shaped (square with one diagonal vertical) with a black legend and border on a yellow background. Warning signs shall be designed in accordance with the sizes, shapes, colors, and legends contained in the “Standard Highway Signs” publication (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a05">Section 1A.05</a>) <span class="ca">and Caltrans’ California Sign Specifications</span>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A warning sign that is larger than the size shown in the Oversized column in <a href="#fig-2c-1">Table 2C-1</a> <span class="ca">and 2C-1(CA)</span> for that particular sign may be diamond-shaped or may be rectangular or square in shape.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The use of a shape other than diamond-shaped is typical for overhead installations.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a05">Section 2A.05</a> contains information on allowable methods to accommodate a diamond-shaped warning sign where the lateral space available in which to install a diamond-shaped warning sign is constrained, such as in urban locations, when mounting on a narrow median barrier or adjacent to a retaining wall, including the display of the standard legend in a vertically oriented rectangle.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The use of LEDs in the border and legend of warning signs is described in <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a12">Section 2A.12</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Except for symbols on warning signs, minor modifications may be made to the design provided that the essential appearance characteristics are met. Modifications may be made to the symbols shown on combined horizontal alignment/intersection signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c09">Section 2C.09</a>) and intersection warning signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c41">Section 2C.41</a>) in order to approximate the geometric configuration of the intersecting roadway(s).</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Word message warning signs other than those provided in this Manual may be developed<span class="ca">by Caltrans (in accordance </span> <span class="ca">with CVC § 21400) </span>and installed by State and <span class="ca">or</span>local highway agencies for conditions otherwise not addressed by standard signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a04">Section 2A.04</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> Warning signs regarding conditions associated with pedestrians, bicyclists, and playgrounds and their related plaques may have a black legend and border on a yellow or fluorescent yellow-green background.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> Warning signs regarding conditions associated with school buses and schools and their related supplemental plaques shall have a black legend and border on a fluorescent yellow-green background (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/7b/#7b01">Section 7B.01</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> Consistent with the provisions of <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4s/#4s03">Section 4S.03</a>, a Warning Beacon may be used in combination with a standard warning sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a09">Section 2A.09</a> for the use of educational plaques. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c03" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.03 Size of Warning Signs and Plaques

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.03 &middot; manual page 308 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.03/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Except as provided in <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a07">Section 2A.07</a>, the sizes for warning signs shall be as shown in <a href="#fig-2c-1">Table 2C-1</a> <span class="ca">and </span> <span class="ca"><a href="#fig-2c-1ca">Table 2C-1(CA)</a></span>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to Paragraph 02 of <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c02">Section 2C.02</a> and Paragraph 06 of this section for sizes larger than shown in <a href="#fig-2c-1">Table 2C-1</a> and Table </span> <span class="ca">2C-1(CA). </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a07">Section 2A.07</a> contains information regarding the applicability of the various columns in <a href="#fig-2c-1">Table 2C-1</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Except as provided in Paragraph 5 of this Section, the minimum size for all diamond-shaped warning signs facing traffic on a multi-lane conventional road where the posted speed limit is higher than 35 mph shall be 36 x 36 inches.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The minimum size for supplemental warning plaques that are not included in <a href="#fig-2c-1">Table 2C-1</a> shall be as shown in <a href="#fig-2c-2">Table 2C-2</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> If a diamond-shaped warning sign is placed on the left-hand side of a multi-lane roadway to supplement the installation of the same warning sign on the right-hand side of the roadway, the minimum size identified in the Single Lane column in <a href="#fig-2c-1">Table 2C-1</a> may be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Signs and plaques larger than those shown in Tables 2C-1 and 2C-2 may be used (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a11">Section 2A.11</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The minimum size for all diamond-shaped warning signs facing traffic on exit and entrance ramps at major interchanges connecting an Expressway or Freeway with an Expressway or Freeway (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2e/#2e11">Section 2E.11</a>) should be the size identified in <a href="#fig-2c-1">Table 2C-1</a> for the mainline roadway classification (Expressway or Freeway). If a minimum size is not provided in the Freeway Column, the Expressway size should be used. If a minimum size is not provided in the Freeway or the Expressway Column, the Oversized size should be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The minimum size for all diamond-shaped warning signs facing traffic on exit and entrance ramps at all other interchanges (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2e/#2e11">Section 2E.11</a>) should be 36 x 36 inches.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The typical size of warning signs used on low-volume rural roads with operating speeds <span class="ca">posted speed limits</span> of 30 mph or less should be in accordance with the minimum column of <a href="#fig-2c-1">Table 2C-1</a>.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c04" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.04 Placement of Warning Signs

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.04 &middot; manual page 309 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.04/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Information on the placement of warning signs is contained in Sections 2A.13 through 2A.18.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The time needed for detection, recognition, decision, and reaction is called the Perception-Response Time (PRT). <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a> is provided as an aid for determining warning sign location. The distances shown in <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a> can be adjusted for roadway features, other signing, and to improve visibility.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Warning signs should be placed so that they provide an adequate PRT. The distances contained in <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a> should be applied with engineering judgment.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Minimum spacing between warning signs with different messages should be based on the estimated PRT for driver comprehension of and reaction to the second sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The effectiveness of the placement of warning signs should be periodically evaluated under both day and night conditions.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Horizontal Alignment Warning Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2c05" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.05 Horizontal Alignment Warning Signs – General

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.05 &middot; manual page 310 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.05/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A variety of horizontal alignment warning signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>), pavement markings (see Chapter 3B), and delineation (see Chapter 3G) can be used to advise motorists of a change in the roadway alignment. Uniform application of these traffic control devices with respect to the amount of change in the roadway alignment conveys a consistent message establishing driver expectancy and promoting effective roadway operations. The design and application of horizontal alignment warning signs to meet those requirements are addressed in Sections 2C.05 through 2C.12.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The following list identifies treatments that might be used in advance of or within a change in horizontal alignment: A.Horizontal alignment (Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-1.svg">W1-1</a>), Curve (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-2.svg">W1-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-10.svg">W1-10</a> series, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-11.svg">W1-11</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-13.svg">W1-13</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-15.svg">W1-15</a>), Reverse Turn (W1- 3), Reverse Curve (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-4.svg">W1-4</a>), Winding Road (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-5.svg">W1-5</a>), Exit Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-2.svg">W13-2</a>), Ramp Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-3.svg">W13-3</a>), and Combination Horizontal Alignment (Advisory Exit or Ramp Speed <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-6.svg">W13-6</a> through <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-11.svg">W13-11</a>)) signs (see Sections 2C.07, 2C.09, and 2C.12) B.Advisory Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1P.svg">W13-1P</a>) plaque (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c59">Section 2C.59</a>) C.Chevron Alignment (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-8.svg">W1-8</a>) signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c08">Section 2C.08</a>) D.Delineators (see Chapter 3G) E.One Direction Large Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-6.svg">W1-6</a>) sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c10">Section 2C.10</a>) F.Raised Retroreflective Pavement Markers (see Sections 3B.15 through 3B.17) G.Sign or marking conspicuity enhancements (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a11">Section 2A.11</a>) H.Wide edge lines (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3a/#3a04">Section 3A.04</a>) I.Pavement word, symbol and arrow markings (symbol or words) (see Sections 3B.20 through 3B.22) J.Rumble strips (see Chapter 3K) K.Vehicle Speed Feedback Sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c13">Section 2C.13</a>) L.Speed reduction markings (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3b/#3b28">Section 3B.28</a>)</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> In addition, considerations other than traffic control devices, such as improved surface friction (high friction surface treatments), pavement edge treatments, lighting improvements, increased superelevation, and rumble strips, might be used in advance of or within a change in horizontal alignment.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Except as provided in <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c06">Section 2C.06</a>, the selection of traffic control devices used to warn road users of a change in horizontal alignment or to provide guidance in navigating the change in horizontal alignment should be based on consideration of one or more of the following factors: A.The speed of traffic on the approach to the change in horizontal alignment B.The recommended advisory speed for the change in horizontal alignment C.The difference between the speed limit and the advisory speed, or the speed differential for the change in horizontal alignment D.Daily traffic volumes on the roadway E.The typical mix of vehicle types on the roadway F.Sight distance throughout the change in horizontal alignment G.Other types of traffic control devices that are used in advance of and within the change in horizontal alignment on the same roadway segment H.The crash history of the change in horizontal alignment I.The presence of driveways or intersections within the curve radius</p>
</div>

<span id="2c06" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.06 Device Selection for Changes in Horizontal Alignment

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.06 &middot; manual page 310 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.06/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The criteria shown in Chart A of <a href="#fig-2c-4">Table 2C-4</a> shall be used to determine the need for devices for changes in horizontal alignment. If the use of a device or devices is indicated by Chart A of <a href="#fig-2c-4">Table 2C-4</a>, then Chart B of <a href="#fig-2c-4">Table 2C-4</a> shall be used to specify the type(s) of devices to be used in advance of, and/or along, a horizontal curve, except as provided in Paragraphs 3, 5, and 6 of this Section. The speed differential in Chart B of Table 2C- 4 shall be the difference between the horizontal curve’s advisory speed and the roadway’s posted speed limit, statutory speed limit, or the 85th percentile speed on the approach to the curve.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Chart A of <a href="#fig-2c-4">Table 2C-4</a> represents existing AADT, type of roadway, and whether or not there are existing markings.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> A One Direction Large Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-6.svg">W1-6</a>) sign may be used in place of or to supplement delineators (see Chapter 3G) or Chevron Alignment (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-8.svg">W1-8</a>) signs when: A.Site conditions limit the number of delineators or Chevron Alignment signs that are visible; or B.The number of delineators or Chevron Alignment signs that can be installed within the change in horizontal alignment is less than the number determined by the spacing specified in Sections 2C.08 or 3G.04.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Additional or supplemental devices may be used for a change in horizontal alignment on the basis of engineering judgment.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Devices for changes in horizontal alignment may be omitted when the speed limit on the approach to an alignment change is 20 mph or less.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Devices for changes in horizontal alignment may be omitted on urban streets with an AADT of 1,000 vehicles per day or less.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> For purposes of selecting traffic control devices for changes in horizontal alignment, an arterial or collector is considered to have pavement markings when either a center line, edge lines, or both are present. Warrants for center lines and edge lines are provided in Sections 3B.02 and 3B.10, respectively.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c07" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.07 Horizontal Alignment Signs (W1-1 through W1-5, W1-11, and W1-15)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.07 &middot; manual page 311 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.07/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> If <a href="#fig-2c-4">Table 2C-4</a> indicates that a horizontal alignment sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) is required, recommended, or allowed, the sign installed in advance of the curve shall be a Curve (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-2.svg">W1-2</a>) sign unless a different sign is recommended or allowed by the provisions of this Section.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-1.svg">W1-1</a>) sign should be used instead of a Curve (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-2.svg">W1-2</a>) sign in advance of a horizontal curve that has an advisory speed of 30 mph or less.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Where there are two changes in roadway alignment in opposite directions that are separated by a tangent distance of less than 600 feet, the Reverse Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-3.svg">W1-3</a>) sign should be used instead of multiple Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-1.svg">W1-1</a>) signs or the Reverse Curve (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-4.svg">W1-4</a>) sign should be used instead of multiple Curve (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-2.svg">W1-2</a>) signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> <a href="#fig-2c-2">Figure 2C-2</a> provides examples of warning signs used for turns and curves.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> A Winding Road (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-5.svg">W1-5</a>) sign may be used instead of multiple Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-1.svg">W1-1</a>) or Curve (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-2.svg">W1-2</a>) signs where there are three or more changes <span class="ca">a series of turns or curves</span>in <span class="ca">a</span> roadway alignment <span class="ca">which requires driving caution, and where curve or </span> <span class="ca">turn signs would be too numerous to be effective.</span>each separated by a tangent distance of less than 600 feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05a</span> <span class="ca">Where any of the curves has an advisory speed that is 10 mph or more below that of the first curve then it should be posted with </span> <span class="ca">a curve or turn sign and an Advisory Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1P.svg">W13-1P</a>) plaque. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> A NEXT XX MILES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3aP.svg">W7-3aP</a>) supplemental distance plaque (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c61">Section 2C.61</a>) may be installed below the Winding Road sign where continuous roadway curves exist for a specific distance.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> If the curve has a change in horizontal alignment of 135 degrees or more, the Hairpin Curve (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-11.svg">W1-11</a>) sign may be used instead of a Turn or Curve sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> If the curve has a change of direction of approximately 270 degrees, such as on a cloverleaf interchange ramp, the 270-degree Loop (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-15.svg">W1-15</a>) sign may be used instead of a Turn or Curve sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> When the Hairpin Curve sign or the 270-degree Loop sign is installed, either a One-Direction Large Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-6.svg">W1-6</a>) sign or Chevron Alignment (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-8.svg">W1-8</a>) signs should be installed on the outside of the turn or curve.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> <span class="ca">Horizontal Alignment Signs may be combined with the Advisory Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1P.svg">W13-1P</a>) plaque (refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c59">Section 2C.59</a>). </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c35">Section 2C.35</a> for use of an oversized square shape version of <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-1.svg">W1-1</a> at a short ramp length with limiting curve near </span> <span class="ca">ramp terminals (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-3">Figure 2C-3</a>). </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c08" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.08 Chevron Alignment Sign (W1-8)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.08 &middot; manual page 312 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.08/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The use of the Chevron Alignment (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-8.svg">W1-8</a>) sign (see Figures 2C-1 and 2C-2) to provide additional emphasis and guidance for a change in horizontal alignment shall be in accordance with the information shown in <a href="#fig-2c-4">Table 2C-4</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Chevron Alignment signs may be used instead of or in addition to standard delineators.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The Chevron Alignment sign shall be a vertical rectangle. No border shall be used on the Chevron Alignment sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If used, Chevron Alignment signs shall be installed on the outside of a turn or curve, in line with and at approximately a right angle to approaching traffic. Chevron Alignment signs shall be installed at a minimum height of 4 feet, measured vertically from the bottom of the sign to the elevation of the near edge of the traveled way.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The approximate spacing of Chevron Alignment signs on the turn or curve measured from the point of curvature (PC) should be as shown in <a href="#fig-2c-5">Table 2C-5</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The Chevron Alignment signs should be visible for a sufficient distance to provide the road user with adequate time to react to the change in alignment.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> LEDs may be used to enhance the conspicuity of Chevron Alignment signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a12">Section 2A.12</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The LEDs used in the Chevron Alignment sign shall consist of yellow LEDs outlining the chevron symbol.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> Chevron Alignment signs shall not be placed on the far side of a T-intersection facing traffic on the stem approach to warn drivers that a through movement is not physically possible, as this is the function of a Two- Direction (or One-Direction) Large Arrow sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> Chevron Alignment signs shall not be used to mark obstructions within or adjacent to the roadway, including the beginning of guardrails or barriers, as this is the function of an object marker (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c70">Section 2C.70</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> Chevron Alignment signs directing traffic to the right shall not be used in the central island of a roundabout or a neighborhood traffic circle.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c09" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.09 Combination Horizontal Alignment/Intersection Signs (W1-10 Series)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.09 &middot; manual page 312 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.09/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-1.svg">W1-1</a>) sign, the Curve (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-2.svg">W1-2</a>) sign, and the Reverse Curve (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-4.svg">W1-4</a>) sign may be combined with the Cross Road (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-1.svg">W2-1</a>) sign or the Side Road (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-2.svg">W2-2</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-3.svg">W2-3</a>) sign to create a combination Horizontal Alignment/Intersection (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-10.svg">W1-10</a> series) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) that depicts the condition where an intersection occurs within or immediately adjacent to a turn or curve.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c65">Section 2C.65</a> contains information about the use of an advance street name plaque to identify an intersecting road.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Elements of the combination Horizontal Alignment/Intersection sign related to horizontal alignment should comply with the provisions of <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c07">Section 2C.07</a>, and elements related to intersection configuration should comply with the provisions of <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c41">Section 2C.41</a>. The symbol design should approximate the configuration of the intersecting roadway(s). No more than one Cross Road or two Side Road symbols should be displayed on any one combination Horizontal Alignment/Intersection sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The use of the combination Horizontal Alignment/Intersection sign shall be in accordance with the provisions of <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c07">Section 2C.07</a> for the appropriate Turn or Curve sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <span class="ca">The advisory speed needs to be determined in accordance with <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c59">Section 2C.59</a>. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c10" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.10 One-Direction Large Arrow Sign (W1-6)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.10 &middot; manual page 313 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.10/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A One-Direction Large Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-6.svg">W1-6</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) may be used either as a supplement or alternative to Chevron Alignment signs or delineators in order to delineate a change in horizontal alignment (see <a href="#fig-2c-2">Figure 2C-2</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A One-Direction Large Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-6.svg">W1-6</a>) sign may be used to supplement a Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-1.svg">W1-1</a>) or Reverse Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-3.svg">W1-3</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-2">Figure 2C-2</a>) to emphasize the abrupt curvature.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The One-Direction Large Arrow sign shall be a horizontal rectangle with an arrow pointing to the left or right.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If used, the One-Direction Large Arrow sign shall be installed on the outside of a turn or curve in line with and at approximately a right angle to approaching traffic.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The One-Direction Large Arrow sign shall not be used where there is no alignment change in the direction of travel, such as at the beginnings and ends of medians or at center piers.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The One-Direction Large Arrow sign directing traffic to the right shall not be used in the central island of a roundabout or a neighborhood traffic circle.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The One-Direction Large Arrow sign should be visible for a sufficient distance to provide the road user with adequate time to react to the change in alignment.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om1-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM1-3.svg">OM1-3</a> object marker should be used below and on the same post as the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-6.svg">W1-6</a> sign. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c70">Section 2C.70</a>. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c59">Section 2C.59</a> for use of the Confirmation Advisory Speed Plaque (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1aP.svg">W13-1aP</a>). </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c11" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.11 Truck Rollover Sign (W1-13)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.11 &middot; manual page 313 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.11/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Truck Rollover (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-13.svg">W1-13</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) may be used as a supplement to a horizontal alignment warning sign to warn drivers of vehicles with a high center of gravity, such as trucks, tankers, and recreational vehicles, of a curve or turn where there are: A.Past incidents of truck rollovers at the specific location, B.High volumes of trucks, or C.A speed differential (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c06">Section 2C.06</a>) that might pose a greater risk for vehicles with high centers of gravity.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Where engineering judgment determines the need for the installation of a Truck Rollover (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-13.svg">W1-13</a>) sign, it should be located downstream of the horizontal alignment warning sign in advance of the curve.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If a Truck Rollover (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-13.svg">W1-13</a>) sign is used, it shall be accompanied by an Advisory Speed (W13- 1P) plaque indicating the recommended speed for vehicles with a higher center of gravity.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03a</span> <span class="ca">An oversized square shape version should be used if installed on the outside of a turn or curve in line with and at approximately </span> <span class="ca">a right angle to approaching traffic (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-3">Figure 2C-3</a>).</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The Truck Rollover sign may include conspicuity enhancements, or may be a blank-out sign, activated by the detection of an approaching vehicle with a high center of gravity that is traveling in excess of the recommended speed for the condition.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The curved arrow on the Truck Rollover sign shows the direction of roadway curvature. The truck tips in the opposite direction.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c12" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.12 Advisory Exit and Ramp Speed Signs (W13-2 and W13-3) and Combination Horizontal Alignment/Advisory Exit and Ramp Speed Signs (W13-6 through W13-13)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.12 &middot; manual page 314 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.12/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Where an advisory speed is posted in advance of a freeway or expressway exit, the Advisory Exit Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-2.svg">W13-2</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) shall be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Where an advisory speed is posted in advance of a conventional road ramp or to another roadway or roadside facility, the Advisory Ramp Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-3.svg">W13-3</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) shall be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> An Advisory Exit Speed or Advisory Ramp Speed sign shall be used when the difference between the mainline roadway speed limit and the exit or ramp advisory speed in the vicinity of the departure is 20 mph or greater.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03a</span> <span class="ca">The Advisory Ramp Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-3.svg">W13-3</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) should be installed on the right of right freeway to freeway </span> <span class="ca">connectors or on the left of left freeway to freeway connector ramps just beyond the neutral area (gore) where the ramps cannot be </span> <span class="ca">comfortably negotiated by motorists at approach speeds. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> An Advisory Exit Speed or Advisory Ramp Speed sign should be used when the difference between the mainline roadway speed limit and the exit or ramp advisory speed in the vicinity of the departure is 15 mph.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> An Advisory Exit Speed or Advisory Ramp Speed sign may be used based on engineering judgment when the difference between the mainline roadway speed limit and the exit or ramp advisory speed in the vicinity of the departure is 10 mph or less.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">05a</span> <span class="ca">Where additional warning is needed for ramp curvature beyond the neutral area (gore), a curve warning sign and an advisory </span> <span class="ca">speed should be posted. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The Combination Horizontal Alignment/Advisory Exit Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-6.svg">W13-6</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-8.svg">W13-8</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-10.svg">W13-10</a>) signs (see Figure 2C- 1) may be used in lieu of the Advisory Exit Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-2.svg">W13-2</a>) sign, and the combination Horizontal Alignment/Advisory Ramp Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-7.svg">W13-7</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-9.svg">W13-9</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-11.svg">W13-11</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) may be used in lieu of the Advisory Ramp Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-3.svg">W13-3</a>) sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The Combination Truck Rollover/Advisory Exit Speed and Truck Rollover/Advisory Ramp Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-12.svg">W13-12</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-13.svg">W13-13</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) may be used in lieu of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-2.svg">W13-2</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-3.svg">W13-3</a> signs respectively if the tip over condition is in the vicinity of the gore.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> Roadway geometrics represented on the Combination Horizontal Alignment/Advisory Exit and Combination Horizontal Alignment/Advisory Ramp Speed signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) shall be limited to the standard signs shown in this Manual.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> If used, the Advisory Exit Speed sign or the Combination Horizontal Alignment/Advisory Exit Speed sign should be installed along the deceleration lane<span class="ca">on the right of right exit ramps or on the left of left exit ramps just beyond the neutral area </span> <span class="ca">(gore)</span>. The Advisory Exit Speed or the Combination Horizontal Alignment/Advisory Exit signs should be visible in time for the road user to decelerate and make an exiting maneuver.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">09a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to FHWA’s List of Known Errors for error in Paragraph 9 text. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a04">Section 1A.04</a> for more details. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">09b</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-2.svg">W13-2</a> sign is not necessary for an exit ramp that has tangent alignment and terminates at a stop sign or a signal. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> Regulatory Speed Limit signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b21">Section 2B.21</a>) should not be located in the vicinity of exit ramps or deceleration lanes, particularly where they will conflict with the advisory speed displayed on the Advisory Exit or Ramp Speed signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c06">Section 2C.06</a> <span class="ca">2C.59 </span>contains provisions for the determination of the displayed advisory speed.</p>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a> lists recommended advance sign placement distances for deceleration to various advisory speeds.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> Where there is a need to remind road users of the recommended advisory speed, a horizontal alignment warning sign with an advisory speed plaque displaying the same advisory speed may be installed at a downstream location along the ramp.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> If the ramp curvature changes to the extent that it warrants a lower advisory speed, a horizontal alignment warning sign with the new advisory speed should be displayed in advance of the change in curvature<span class="ca">(refer to <a href="#fig-2c-3">Figure 2C-3</a>)</span>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> The One-Direction Large Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-6.svg">W1-6</a>) sign may be installed beyond the exit gore on the outside of the curve to provide additional warning of an immediate change in curvature. When used in conjunction with the exit speed, the One-Direction Large Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-6.svg">W1-6</a>) sign may be supplemented with a Confirmation Advisory Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1aP.svg">W13-1aP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) when the plaque is not used with the Exit Gore (E5-1 series) sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> The horizontal alignment symbol displayed on the Combination Horizontal Alignment/Advisory Exit and Ramp Speed signs should be consistent with the horizontal geometry of the ramp.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> Examples of advisory speed signing for exit ramps are shown in <a href="#fig-2c-3">Figure 2C-3</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> <span class="ca">Refer to FHWA’s List of Known Errors for error in Paragraph 17 text. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a04">Section 1A.04</a> for more details. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c13" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.13 Vehicle Speed Feedback Sign and Plaque (W13-20 and W13-20aP)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.13 &middot; manual page 315 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.13/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Vehicle Speed Feedback (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-20/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-20.svg">W13-20</a>) sign or (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-20ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-20aP.svg">W13-20aP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-4">Figure 2C-4</a>) that displays the speed of an approaching vehicle to the vehicle operator may be used to provide warning to drivers of their speed in relation to either a speed limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-1.svg">R2-1</a>) sign or a horizontal alignment warning sign assembly with a posted advisory speed.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> When used to display the speed of an approaching vehicle in relation to the posted speed limit, the Vehicle Speed Feedback (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-20ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-20aP.svg">W13-20aP</a>) plaque shall be mounted below a Speed Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-1.svg">R2-1</a>) sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b21">Section 2B.21</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> When used to supplement a horizontal alignment warning sign advisory speed, the Vehicle Speed Feedback (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-20/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-20.svg">W13-20</a>) sign shall be an independent installation near the point of curvature of a horizontal curve (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c06">Section 2C.06</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The legend YOUR SPEED shall be a black legend on a yellow retroreflective background, except as provided in Sections 6H.01 and 7B.01. The changeable legend displaying the speed of the approaching vehicle shall be a yellow luminous legend on a black opaque background. The vehicle speed displayed on the changeable portion of the sign shall be displayed as an integer. The Vehicle Speed Feedback sign and plaque shall not flash, strobe, change color, or use other animated elements integrated into the changeable legend display. When no vehicles are approaching, the changeable display shall not display a legend.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The changeable portion of the Vehicle Speed Feedback legend should be approximately the same height, width, and stroke of those on the Speed Limit sign it supplements or is mounted below.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> When a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-20ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-20aP.svg">W13-20aP</a> plaque is used with a Speed Limit sign it should be approximately the same width as the Speed Limit sign it is mounted below.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Vertical Grade Warning Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2c14" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.14 Hill Signs (W7-1 and W7-1a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.14 &middot; manual page 316 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.14/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Hill (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-1.svg">W7-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a>) should be used in advance of a downgrade where the length, percent of grade, horizontal curvature, and/or other physical features require special precautions on the part of road users.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The Hill sign and supplemental grade (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3P.svg">W7-3P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a> and <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c64">Section 2C.64</a>) used in combination, or the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-1a.svg">W7-1a</a> sign used alone, should be installed in advance of downgrades for the following conditions: A.5% grade that is more than 3,000 feet in length, B.6% grade that is more than 2,000 feet in length, C.7% grade that is more than 1,000 feet in length, D.8% grade that is more than 750 feet in length, or E.9% grade that is more than 500 feet in length.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> These signs should also be installed for steeper grades or where crash experience and field observations indicate a need.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Supplemental plaques (see Sections 2C.57 and 2C.64) and larger signs should be used for emphasis or where special hill characteristics exist. On longer grades, the use of the Hill sign with a distance (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3aP.svg">W7-3aP</a>) plaque or the combination distance/grade (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3bP.svg">W7-3bP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a>) at periodic intervals of approximately 1-mile spacing should be considered.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> A USE LOW GEAR (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-2p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-2P.svg">W7-2P</a>) or TRUCKS USE LOWER GEAR (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-2bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-2bP.svg">W7-2bP</a>) supplemental plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a>) may be used to indicate a situation where downshifting as well as braking might be advisable.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> <span class="ca">The SLOW TRUCKS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w51ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w51ca.webp">W51(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-5ca">Figure 2C-5(CA)</a>) may be used to inform drivers that slow moving trucks </span> <span class="ca">substantially interfere with the flow of traffic. The Next Distance (W7-3a) plaque may be used with the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w51ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w51ca.webp">W51(CA)</a> sign. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> <span class="ca">The WATCH DOWNHILL SPEED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw4-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw4-1ca.webp">SW4-1(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-5ca">Figure 2C-5(CA)</a>) may be used on long downhill grades to remind </span> <span class="ca">motorists to maintain the posted speed. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c15" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.15 Truck Escape Ramp Signs (W7-4 Series)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.15 &middot; manual page 316 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.15/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Where applicable, truck escape (or runaway truck) ramp advance warning signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a>) should be located approximately 1 mile and approximately ½ mile in advance of the grade, and of the escape ramp. An additional <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-4b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-4b.svg">W7-4b</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-4c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-4c.svg">W7-4c</a> sign should be placed at the gore.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A RUNAWAY VEHICLES ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-10.svg">R4-10</a>) sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b41">Section 2B.41</a>) should be installed near the escape ramp entrance to discourage other road users from entering the ramp. No Parking (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-3.svg">R8-3</a>) signs should be placed near the ramp entrance.<span class="ca"> NO STOPPING ANYTIME (R26A(S)(CA)) signs (refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b41">Section 2B.41</a>) should be placed to keep motorists from stopping </span> <span class="ca">in the path of runaway trucks.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> When truck escape ramps are installed, at least one of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-4.svg">W7-4</a> series signs shall be used.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> A SAND (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-4dp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-4dP.svg">W7-4dP</a>), GRAVEL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-4ep/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-4eP.svg">W7-4eP</a>), or PAVED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-4fp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-4fP.svg">W7-4fP</a>) supplemental plaque (see Figure 2C- 5) may be used to describe the ramp surface. State and local highway agencies <span class="ca">Caltrans (in accordance with CVC § 21400, refer to Section </span> <span class="ca">2A.04) </span>may develop appropriate word message signs for the specific situation.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <span class="ca">The DEEP GRAVEL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w30bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w30bca.webp">W30B(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-5ca">Figure 2C-5(CA)</a>) shall be placed on all truck escape ramps. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w30bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w30bca.webp">W30B(CA)</a> sign should be placed near the outside edge of the paved ramp prior to the beginning of the gravel bed. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> <span class="ca">The RIGHT (LEFT) EXIT (W30cP(CA)) plaque (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-5ca">Figure 2C-5(CA)</a>) should be used to indicate a right or left exit to a truck </span> <span class="ca">escape ramp. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> <span class="ca">Erect the W30cP(CA) plaque below and on the same post with the first <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-4.svg">W7-4</a> sign. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="#fig-3g-103ca">Figure 3G-103(CA)</a> for Runaway Truck Ramp sign and marking details. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c16" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.16 HILL BLOCKS VIEW Sign (W7-6)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.16 &middot; manual page 317 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.16/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A HILL BLOCKS VIEW (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-6.svg">W7-6</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a>) may be used on the approach to a crest vertical curve where the vertical curvature provides inadequate stopping sight distance at the posted speed limit.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> When a vertical curve results in a sight distance obstruction to a specific condition beyond the crest of the vertical curve, the warning sign for the specific condition beyond the vertical crest should be used rather than the HILL BLOCKS VIEW sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> When a HILL BLOCKS VIEW sign is used, it should be supplemented by an Advisory Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1P.svg">W13-1P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) indicating the recommended speed for traveling over the hillcrest based on available stopping sight distance.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Roadway Geometry Warning Signs</div>


<span id="2c17" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.17 ROAD NARROWS Sign (W5-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.17 &middot; manual page 318 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.17/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Except as provided in Paragraph 2 of this Section, a ROAD NARROWS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w5-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W5-1.svg">W5-1</a>) sign (see Figure 2C- 6) should be used in advance of a transition on two-lane roads where the pavement width is reduced abruptly to a width such that vehicles traveling in opposite directions cannot simultaneously travel through the narrow portion of the roadway without reducing speed.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The ROAD NARROWS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w5-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W5-1.svg">W5-1</a>) sign may be omitted on low-volume local streets that have speed limits of 30 mph or less.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Additional emphasis may be provided by the use of object markers and delineators (see Sections 2C.70 through 2C.73 and Chapter 3G). The Advisory Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1P.svg">W13-1P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a> and <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c59">Section 2C.59</a>) may be used to indicate the recommended speed.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c18" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.18 NARROW BRIDGE and NARROW UNDERPASS Signs (W5-2 and W5-2a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.18 &middot; manual page 318 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.18/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A NARROW BRIDGE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w5-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W5-2.svg">W5-2</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) should be used in advance of any bridge or culvert having a two-way roadway horizontal clearance of 16 to 18 feet, or any bridge or culvert having a roadway horizontal clearance less than the width of the approach travel lanes. Where these conditions exist for an underpass, a NARROW UNDERPASS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w5-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W5-2a.svg">W5-2a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) should be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Additional emphasis should be provided by the use of object markers, delineators, and/or pavement markings.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> A NARROW BRIDGE sign may be used in advance of a bridge or culvert on which the approach shoulders are narrowed or eliminated. Where these conditions exist for an underpass, a NARROW UNDERPASS sign may be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The NARROW BRIDGE or NARROW UNDERPASS sign may be omitted on low-volume rural roads where there is adequate sight distance to the bridge, culvert, or underpass on both approaches.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="#fig-3g-104ca">Figure 3G-104(CA)</a> for narrow bridge sign and marking details. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> <span class="ca">The TUNNEL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw37ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw37ca.webp">SW37(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-6ca">Figure 2C-6(CA)</a>) may be used to warn road user that there is a tunnel ahead. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c19" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.19 ONE LANE BRIDGE and ONE LANE UNDERPASS Signs (W5-3 and W5-3a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.19 &middot; manual page 318 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.19/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A ONE LANE BRIDGE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w5-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W5-3.svg">W5-3</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) should be used on two-way roadways in advance of any bridge or culvert: A.Having a roadway horizontal clearance of less than 16 feet, or B.Having a roadway horizontal clearance of less than 18 feet when commercial vehicles constitute a high proportion of the traffic, or C.Having a roadway horizontal clearance of 18 feet or less where the sight distance on the approach is less than that shown in Condition A of <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Where these conditions exist for an underpass, a ONE LANE UNDERPASS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w5-3a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W5-3a.svg">W5-3a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) should be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Additional emphasis should be provided by the use of object markers, delineators, and/or pavement markings.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The ONE LANE BRIDGE or ONE LANE UNDERPASS sign may be omitted on low-volume rural roads where there is adequate sight distance to the bridge, culvert, or underpass on both approaches.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R1-1.svg">R1-1</a>) or YIELD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r1-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R1-2.svg">R1-2</a>) signs (see Sections 2B.04 and 2B.05) and related pavement markings (see Sections 3B.21 and 3B.22) may be used when conditions A, B, or C in Paragraph 1 of this Section apply.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c20" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.20 Divided Highway Sign (W6-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.20 &middot; manual page 319 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.20/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Divided Highway (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-1.svg">W6-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) should be used on the approaches to a section of highway (not an intersection or junction) where the opposing flows of traffic are separated by a median or other physical barrier.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The Divided Highway (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-1.svg">W6-1</a>) sign shall not be used instead of a Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a> series) sign on the approach end of a median island.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c21" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.21 Divided Highway Ends Sign (W6-2)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.21 &middot; manual page 319 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.21/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Divided Highway Ends (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-2.svg">W6-2</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) should be used in advance of the end of a section of physically divided highway (not an intersection or junction) as a warning of two-way traffic ahead.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The Two-Way Traffic (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-3.svg">W6-3</a>) sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c51">Section 2C.51</a>) should be used to give warning and notice of the transition to a two-lane, two-way section.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c22" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.22 Freeway or Expressway Ends Signs (W19 Series)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.22 &middot; manual page 319 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.22/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A FREEWAY ENDS XX MILES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-1.svg">W19-1</a>) sign or a FREEWAY ENDS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-3.svg">W19-3</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) may be used in advance of the end of a freeway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">At problem locations, a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-1.svg">W19-1</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-3.svg">W19-3</a> sign may include dual installations. Yellow flashing beacons may be considered. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01b</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b109ca">Section 2B.109(CA)</a> for the END FREEWAY (R58(CA)) sign. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> An EXPRESSWAY ENDS XX MILES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-2.svg">W19-2</a>) sign or an EXPRESSWAY ENDS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-4.svg">W19-4</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) may be used in advance of the end of an expressway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The rectangular <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-1.svg">W19-1</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-2.svg">W19-2</a> signs may be post-mounted or may be mounted overhead for increased emphasis.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If the reason that the freeway is ending is that the next portion of the freeway is not yet constructed and as a result all traffic must use an exit ramp to leave the freeway, an ALL TRAFFIC MUST EXIT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-5.svg">W19-5</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) should be used in addition to the Freeway Ends signs in advance of the downstream end of the freeway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <span class="ca">A distance plaque may be used with a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-3.svg">W19-3</a> or a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w19-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W19-4.svg">W19-4</a> sign.</span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c23" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.23 Double Arrow Sign (W12-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.23 &middot; manual page 319 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.23/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-1.svg">W12-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) may be used to advise road users that traffic is permitted to pass on either side of an island, obstruction, or gore in the roadway. Traffic separated by this sign may either rejoin or change directions.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used on an island, the Double Arrow sign should be mounted near the approach end.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If used in front of a pier or obstruction, the Double Arrow sign should be mounted on the face of, or just in front of, the pier or obstruction. Where stripe markings are used on the pier or obstruction, they should be discontinued to leave a 3-inch space around the outside of the Double Arrow sign.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c24" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.24 DEAD END, NO OUTLET, and ROAD ENDS Signs (W14-1, W14-1a, W14-2, W14-2a, W8-26, and W8-26a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.24 &middot; manual page 319 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.24/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The DEAD END (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-1.svg">W14-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) may be used at the entrance to a single road or street that terminates without intersecting another street. The NO OUTLET (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-2.svg">W14-2</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) may be used at the entrance to a road or road network from which there is no other exit.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> DEAD END (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-1a.svg">W14-1a</a>) or NO OUTLET (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-2a.svg">W14-2a</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) may be used in combination with Street Name (D3-1) signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2d/#2d45">Section 2D.45</a>) to warn turning traffic that the cross street ends in the direction indicated by the arrow.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> At locations where the cross street does not have a name, a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-1a.svg">W14-1a</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-2a.svg">W14-2a</a> sign may be used alone in place of a street name sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> When the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-1.svg">W14-1</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-2.svg">W14-2</a> sign is used, the sign should be posted as near as practicable to the entry point or at a sufficient advance distance to permit the road user to avoid the dead end or no outlet condition by turning at the nearest intersecting street.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The DEAD END (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-1a.svg">W14-1a</a>) or NO OUTLET (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-2a.svg">W14-2a</a>) sign shall not be used instead of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-1.svg">W14-1</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-2.svg">W14-2</a> signs where traffic can proceed straight through the intersection into the dead end street or no outlet area.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The ROAD ENDS XX FT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-26/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-26.svg">W8-26</a>) or STREET ENDS XX FT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-26a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-26a.svg">W8-26a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) may be used on the approach to the end of a conventional road or street where the terminus is not apparent.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06a</span> <span class="ca">The Road Ends XX FT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-26/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-26.svg">W8-26</a>) or STREET ENDS XX FT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-26a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-26a.svg">W8-26a</a>) sign may be used in advance of the END (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w31ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w31ca.webp">W31(CA)</a>) sign in </span> <span class="ca">combination with an end-of-roadway marker. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c73">Section 2C.73</a>.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Information about the use of Type 4 object markers to mark the end of the road or street is contained in <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c73">Section 2C.73</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-26/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-26.svg">W8-26</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-26a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-26a.svg">W8-26a</a> signs shall not be used in place of a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-1.svg">W14-1</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-2.svg">W14-2</a> sign at the entrance to such a road or street.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c22">Section 2C.22</a> contains information on signs for use on the approach to the end of a freeway or expressway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> <span class="ca">The END (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w31ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w31ca.webp">W31(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-6ca">Figure 2C-6(CA)</a>) may be used where a street or highway ends. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> <span class="ca">Install the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w31ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w31ca.webp">W31(CA)</a> sign in a head-on position (left side) in combination with an end-of-roadway marker. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c73">Section 2C.73</a>. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c25" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.25 Low Clearance Signs (W12-2, W12-2a, and W12-2b)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.25 &middot; manual page 320 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.25/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Low Clearance Advance (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2.svg">W12-2</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) shall be used to warn road users of vertical clearances less than 14 <span class="ca">15</span> feet 6 inches, or vertical clearances less than 12 inches above the statutory maximum vehicle height, whichever is greater.</p>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">The Low Clearance Advance (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2.svg">W12-2</a>) sign shall be used to warn motorists of low structure clearances. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">01b</span> <span class="ca">For clearance 15 feet 6 inch or less, in addition to the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2a.svg">W12-2a</a> sign, two Low Clearance Advance (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2.svg">W12-2</a>) signs shall be </span> <span class="ca">installed on the right side of the roadway.
