The first <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2.svg">W12-2</a> sign shall be placed in advance of the nearest intersecting street </span> <span class="ca">or highway or wide point in the road at which a motorist can detour or safely turn around. See paragraph 04. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">01c</span> <span class="ca">The second <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2.svg">W12-2</a> sign shall be placed in advance of the structure. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The actual clearance should <span class="ca">shall</span>be displayed on the Low Clearance (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2.svg">W12-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2a.svg">W12-2a</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2b.svg">W12-2b</a>) sign to the nearest 1 inch not exceeding the actual clearance. However, in areas that experience changes in temperature causing frost action, a reduction, not exceeding 3 inches, should be used for this condition.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Clearances should be evaluated periodically to determine if additional low clearance signing is necessary, particularly when resurfacing operations have occurred, on routes onto which over-height vehicles are normally directed under the permit process, and structures that are susceptible to catastrophic failure when struck by over-height vehicles.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2.svg">W12-2</a> sign with a supplemental distance plaque should also be placed at the nearest intersecting road or wide point in the road at which a vehicle can detour or turn around.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Where there is a need to warn of a low clearance on an intersecting road or off a freeway or expressway exit, a rectangular warning sign with an appropriate word legend should be used rather than a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2.svg">W12-2</a> sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05a</span> <span class="ca">The CAUTION, VERTICAL CLEARANCE ___’ ___” Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w34cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w34cca.webp">W34C(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-6ca">Figure 2C-6(CA)</a>) shall be used on all </span> <span class="ca">blind approaches to structures with clearances 15 feet 6 inch or less.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05b</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w34cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w34cca.webp">W34C(CA)</a> sign is used to warn motorists of low structure clearance around corners. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">05c</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w34cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w34cca.webp">W34C(CA)</a> sign should be placed at a location where the motorist can detour or safely turn around before making the turn. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The Low Clearance Overhead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2a.svg">W12-2a</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2b.svg">W12-2b</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) may be installed on the structure to supplement the advance warning sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">06a</span> <span class="ca">The Low Clearance Overhead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2a.svg">W12-2a</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2b.svg">W12-2b</a>) sign shall be used to warn motorists of structural clearance 15 feet 6 </span> <span class="ca">inch or less. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06b</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2a.svg">W12-2a</a> sign should be centered over the traveled way on the approach side of all underpasses, overheads, viaducts, </span> <span class="ca">overcrossings, undercrossings, and grade separations for State highways. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">06c</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2a.svg">W12-2a</a> sign shall not encroach over the shoulder area. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> In cases where physical conditions on a structure limit the width such that the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2a.svg">W12-2a</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2b.svg">W12-2b</a> signs are physically unable to fit, a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2.svg">W12-2</a> sign may be installed overhead on the structure or post-mounted in front of the structure, in addition to the required <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2.svg">W12-2</a> sign at the advance location.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> In the case of an arch, or other structure under which the clearance varies greatly, two or more Low Clearance Overhead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2a.svg">W12-2a</a> or 12-2b) signs should be installed on the structure itself to give information as to the clearances over the low clearance portions of the roadway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> If used, the Low Clearance Overhead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2b.svg">W12-2b</a>) sign shall be placed over a lane or shoulder to indicate the portion of the structure with low clearance if the posted clearance does not apply to the entire structure.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> The clearance shown on the Low Clearance Advance sign should <span class="ca">shall</span>match the clearance on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2a.svg">W12-2a</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2b.svg">W12-2b</a> sign or, if there are multiple <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2b.svg">W12-2b</a> signs, shall match the lowest clearance.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> <span class="ca">The clearance shown on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w34cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w34cca.webp">W34C(CA)</a> sign shall match the clearance on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2a.svg">W12-2a</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2b.svg">W12-2b</a> sign or, if there are </span> <span class="ca">multiple <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w12-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W12-2b.svg">W12-2b</a> signs, shall match the lowest clearance. </span></p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Roadway And Weather-Condition Warning Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2c26" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.26 BUMP and DIP Signs (W8-1 and W8-2)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.26 &middot; manual page 322 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.26/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> BUMP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-1.svg">W8-1</a>) and DIP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-2.svg">W8-2</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) should be used in advance of a sharp rise or depression in the profile of the road.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">When used at a cattle guard, the BUMP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-1.svg">W8-1</a>) or DIP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-2.svg">W8-2</a>) signs shall be supplemented with a diagonal downward </span> <span class="ca">pointing arrow (W16-7p) plaque showing the location of the cattle guard.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> These signs may be supplemented with an Advisory Speed plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a> and <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c59">Section 2C.59</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The DIP sign should not be used in advance of a short stretch of depressed alignment that might momentarily hide a vehicle.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> A short stretch of depressed alignment that might momentarily hide a vehicle should be treated as a no-passing zone when center line striping is provided on a two-lane or three-lane road (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3b/#3b03">Section 3B.03</a>).</p>
</div>

<span id="2c27" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.27 SPEED HUMP Sign (W17-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.27 &middot; manual page 322 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.27/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The SPEED HUMP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w17-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W17-1.svg">W17-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) should be used in advance of a vertical deflection in the roadway that is designed to limit the speed of traffic.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the SPEED HUMP sign should be supplemented by an Advisory Speed plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a> and <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c59">Section 2C.59</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If a series of speed humps exists in close proximity, an Advisory Speed plaque may be eliminated on all but the first SPEED HUMP sign in the series.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The legend SPEED BUMP may be used instead of the legend SPEED HUMP on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w17-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W17-1.svg">W17-1</a> sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04a</span> <span class="ca">If a series of speed humps exist in close proximity, a SPEED HUMPS AHEAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w84ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w84ca.webp">W84(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-7ca">Figure 2C-7(CA)</a>) may </span> <span class="ca">replace the first SPEED HUMP sign in the series,</span><span class="ca">provided additional warning of speed humps are provided through signs or </span> <span class="ca">pavement markings at the speed humps. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">04b</span> <span class="ca">If speed humps exist on a network of streets within an area accessible by a limited number of access points to the area, an </span> <span class="ca">optional SPEED HUMP AREA (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w85ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w85ca.webp">W85(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-7ca">Figure 2C-7(CA)</a>) may be placed at each access point to the area, provided </span> <span class="ca">additional warning of speed humps are provided through signs or markings at the speed humps. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Speed humps generally provide more gradual vertical deflection than speed bumps. Speed bumps limit the speed of traffic more severely than speed humps. Other forms of speed humps include speed tables and raised crosswalks or intersections. However, these differences in engineering terminology are not well known by the public, so for signing purposes these terms are interchangeable.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Sections 3B.29 and 3B.30 contain information about the use of markings at and in advance of speed humps.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c28" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.28 PAVEMENT ENDS Sign (W8-3)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.28 &middot; manual page 322 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.28/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A PAVEMENT ENDS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-3.svg">W8-3</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) should be used where a paved surface changes to either a gravel treated surface or an earth road surface.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> An Advisory Speed plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a> and <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c59">Section 2C.59</a>) may be used when the change in roadway condition requires a reduced speed.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c29" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.29 Shoulder Signs (W8-4, W8-9, W8-17, W8-23, and W8-25)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.29 &middot; manual page 322 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.29/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The SOFT SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-4.svg">W8-4</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn of a soft shoulder condition.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The LOW SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-9.svg">W8-9</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn of a shoulder condition where there is an elevation difference of 3 inches or less between the shoulder and the travel lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02a</span> <span class="ca">The black on yellow background LOW SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-9.svg">W8-9</a>) sign shall not be used on State highways. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02b</span> <span class="ca">The low shoulder condition (elevation difference up to 3 inches) between shoulder and the travel lane) is not treated as a </span> <span class="ca">permanent condition on State highways. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">02c</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/6h/#6h26">Section 6H.26</a> for use of <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-9.svg">W8-9</a> on state highway construction zones </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The Shoulder Drop Off (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-17/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-17.svg">W8-17</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) should be used where an unprotected shoulder drop-off, adjacent to the travel lane, exceeds 3 inches in depth for a significant continuous length along the roadway, based on engineering judgment.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> A SHOULDER DROP-OFF (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-17p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-17P.svg">W8-17P</a>) supplemental plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be mounted below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-17/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-17.svg">W8-17</a> sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The NO SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-23/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-23.svg">W8-23</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn road users that a shoulder does not exist along a portion of the roadway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The SHOULDER ENDS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-25/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-25.svg">W8-25</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn road users that a shoulder is ending.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Additional shoulder signs should be placed at appropriate intervals along the road where the condition continually exists.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c30" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.30 Surface Condition Signs (W8-5, W8-7, W8-8, W8-11, W8-13, and W8-14)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.30 &middot; manual page 323 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.30/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Slippery When Wet (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-5.svg">W8-5</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn of unexpected slippery conditions. Supplemental plaques (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) with legends such as ICE, WHEN WET, STEEL DECK, or EXCESS OIL may be used with the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-5.svg">W8-5</a> sign to indicate the reason that the slippery conditions might be present.</p>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">The slippery When Wet (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-5.svg">W8-5</a>) sign may be used with the SNOW (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw46pca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw46pca.webp">SW46P(CA)</a>) plaque to warn road users of conditions where </span> <span class="ca">snow may be on the roadway surface, but chains are not yet required. The combination may be placed in advance of areas where </span> <span class="ca">such conditions may exist, and intermittently as needed where such conditions may exist for long sections of highways. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">01b</span> <span class="ca">The sign combination may be displayed when weather conditions are such that it would be reasonable to assume that snow on </span> <span class="ca">the roadway would be a possibility. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01c</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c01">Section 2C.01</a> for information on when the seasonal snow condition no longer exists. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01d</span> <span class="ca">When used at a cattle guard, the Slippery When Wet (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-5.svg">W8-5</a>) signs shall be supplemented with a diagonal downward </span> <span class="ca">pointing arrow (W16-7p) plaque showing the location of the cattle guard. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The LOOSE GRAVEL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-7.svg">W8-7</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn of loose gravel on the roadway surface.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The ROUGH ROAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-8.svg">W8-8</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn of a rough roadway surface. <span class="ca">It may be </span> <span class="ca">desirable to supplement this sign with an Advisory Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1P.svg">W13-1P</a>) plaque. Where the rough road is 1 mile or more in length, the </span> <span class="ca"><a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-8.svg">W8-8</a> sign may be supplemented with a Next Distance (W7-3a) plaque.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> An UNEVEN LANES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-11.svg">W8-11</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn of a difference in elevation between travel lanes.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The BRIDGE ICES BEFORE ROAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-13.svg">W8-13</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used in advance of bridges to advise bridge users of winter weather conditions. The BRIDGE ICES BEFORE ROAD sign may be removed or covered during seasons of the year when its message is not relevant.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The FALLEN ROCKS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-14/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-14.svg">W8-14</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used in advance of an area that is adjacent to a hillside, mountain, or cliff where rocks frequently fall onto the roadway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> When used, Surface Condition signs should be placed in advance of the beginning of the affected section (see <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a>), and additional signs should be placed at appropriate intervals along the road where the condition exists.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> <span class="ca">The SLIDE AREA (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w38ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w38ca.webp">W38(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-7ca">Figure 2C-7(CA)</a>) may be used in advance of where slides on the highway could be </span> <span class="ca">expected. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> <span class="ca">The SNOW SLIDE AREA (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw41ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw41ca.webp">SW41(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-7ca">Figure 2C-7(CA)</a>) may be used in areas of known snow slide or avalanche </span> <span class="ca">activity. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> <span class="ca">The Next Distance (W7-3a) plaque may be used below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w38ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w38ca.webp">W38(CA)</a>, W50-1(CA) and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw41ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw41ca.webp">SW41(CA)</a> signs. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> <span class="ca">The DRIFTING SAND (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw32ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw32ca.webp">SW32(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-7ca">Figure 2C-7(CA)</a>) may be used to warn traffic of drifting sand on the roadway. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c31" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.31 Warning Signs and Plaque for Motorcyclists (W8-15, W8-15aP, and W8-16)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.31 &middot; manual page 324 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.31/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The signs and plaques described in this Section are intended to give motorcyclists advance notice of surface conditions that might adversely affect their ability to maintain control of their motorcycle under wet or dry conditions. The use of some of the advance surface condition warning signs described in <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c30">Section 2C.30</a>, such as Slippery When Wet, LOOSE GRAVEL, or ROUGH ROAD, can also be helpful to motorcyclists if those conditions exist.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If a portion of a street or highway features a roadway pavement surface that is grooved or textured instead of smooth, such as a grooved skid resistance treatment for a horizontal curve or a brick pavement surface, a GROOVED PAVEMENT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-15.svg">W8-15</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to provide advance warning of this condition to motorcyclists, bicyclists, and other road users. Alternate legends such as TEXTURED PAVEMENT or BRICK PAVEMENT may also be used on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-15.svg">W8-15</a> sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If a bridge or a portion of a bridge includes a metal or grated surface, a METAL BRIDGE DECK (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-16/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-16.svg">W8-16</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to provide advance warning of this condition to motorcyclists, bicyclists, and other road users.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> A Motorcycle (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-15ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-15aP.svg">W8-15aP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be mounted below or above a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-15.svg">W8-15</a> or W8- 16 sign if the warning is intended to be directed primarily to motorcyclists.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c32" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.32 NO CENTER LINE Sign (W8-12)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.32 &middot; manual page 324 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.32/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The NO CENTER LINE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-12.svg">W8-12</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn of a roadway without center line pavement markings.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c33" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.33 NO TRAFFIC SIGNS Sign (W18-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.33 &middot; manual page 324 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.33/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The NO TRAFFIC SIGNS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w18-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W18-1.svg">W18-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-6">Figure 2C-6</a>) may be used only on low-volume rural roads to advise road users that no signs are installed along the distance of the road. The sign may be installed at the point where road users would enter the low volume road or where, based on engineering judgment, the road user might need this information.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3aP.svg">W7-3aP</a> (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a>), <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-2p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-2P.svg">W16-2P</a> (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>), or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-9p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-9P.svg">W16-9P</a> (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) supplemental plaque with the legend NEXT XX MILES, XX FEET, or AHEAD may be installed below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w18-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W18-1.svg">W18-1</a> sign when appropriate.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c34" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.34 Weather Condition Signs (W8-18, W8-19, W8-21, and W8-22)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.34 &middot; manual page 324 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.34/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The ROAD MAY FLOOD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-18.svg">W8-18</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn road users that a section of roadway is subject to frequent flooding. A Depth Gauge (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-19/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-19.svg">W8-19</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may also be installed within a roadway section that frequently floods.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the Depth Gauge sign should be in addition to the ROAD MAY FLOOD sign and should be mounted at the appropriate height to indicate the depth of the water at the deepest point on the roadway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02c</span> <span class="ca">The FLASH FLOOD AREA (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw35ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw35ca.webp">SW35(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-7ca">Figure 2C-7(CA)</a>) may be used in advance of depressions in the highway </span> <span class="ca">alignment that are subject to flash flooding. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The GUSTY WINDS AREA (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-21/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-21.svg">W8-21</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn road users that wind gusts frequently occur along a section of highway that are strong enough to impact the stability of trucks, recreational vehicles, and other vehicles with high centers of gravity. A NEXT XX MILES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3aP.svg">W7-3aP</a>) supplemental plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a>) may be mounted below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-21/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-21.svg">W8-21</a> sign to inform road users of the length of roadway that frequently experiences strong wind gusts.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The FOG AREA (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-22/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-22.svg">W8-22</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-7">Figure 2C-7</a>) may be used to warn road users that foggy conditions frequently reduce visibility along a section of highway. A NEXT XX MILES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3aP.svg">W7-3aP</a>) supplemental plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a>) may be mounted below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-22/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-22.svg">W8-22</a> sign to inform road users of the length of roadway that frequently experiences foggy conditions.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Chapter 2L contains provisions for the use of blank-out or changeable message signs that can be activated by detection of the applicable condition.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> <span class="ca">Refer to Chapter 6O for additional flooding signs. </span></p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Traffic Control And Intersection Warning Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2c35" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.35 Advance Traffic Control Signs (W3-1, W3-2, W3-3, and W3-4)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.35 &middot; manual page 326 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.35/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Stop Ahead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-1.svg">W3-1</a>), Yield Ahead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-2.svg">W3-2</a>), and Signal Ahead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-3.svg">W3-3</a>) Advance Traffic Control signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-8">Figure 2C-8</a>) shall be installed on an approach to a primary traffic control device that is not visible for a sufficient distance to permit the road user to respond to the device (see <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a>). The visibility criteria for a traffic control signal shall be based on having a continuous view of at least two signal faces for the distance specified in <a href="#fig-4d-2">Table 4D-2</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Where intermittent obstructions occur, engineering judgment should determine the treatment to be implemented.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> <a href="#fig-2a-4">Figure 2A-4</a> shows examples of the typical placement of an Advance Traffic Control sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Permanent obstructions causing the limited visibility might include roadway alignment or structures. Intermittent obstructions might include foliage or parked vehicles.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> An Advance Traffic Control sign may be used for additional emphasis of the primary traffic control device, even when the visibility distance to the device is satisfactory.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c65">Section 2C.65</a> contains information about the use of an advance street name plaque to identify an intersecting road.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> A BE PREPARED TO STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-4.svg">W3-4</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-8">Figure 2C-8</a>) may be used to warn of stopped traffic caused by a traffic control signal <span class="ca">or a STOP sign</span>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> A Warning Beacon (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4s/#4s03">Section 4S.03</a>) or yellow LEDs within the border of the sign may be used with an Advance Traffic Control or BE PREPARED TO STOP sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08a</span> <span class="ca">Yellow flashing beacons may be used with Signal Ahead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-3.svg">W3-3</a>) signs in advance of: </span> <span class="ca">A. An isolated traffic signal on either a conventional highway or on an expressway in a rural area. </span> <span class="ca">B. The first traffic signal approaching an urban area. </span> <span class="ca">C. Any traffic signal with limited approach visibility, or where approach speeds exceed 50 mph. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">08b</span> <span class="ca">On divided highways where the median is 8 feet wide, or greater, the installation may consist of: </span> <span class="ca">A. Two Type 1 standards, each with a Signal Ahead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-3.svg">W3-3</a>) sign and a 12-inch signal face, with one standard located in the </span> <span class="ca">median and the other off of the right shoulder; or </span> <span class="ca">B. A Type 9 cantilever flashing beacon installation with a Signal Ahead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-3.svg">W3-3</a>) sign and two 12-inch signal faces as shown in </span> <span class="ca">Caltrans’ Standard Plans. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a05">Section 1A.05</a> for information regarding this publication.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> When a BE PREPARED TO STOP sign is used in advance of a traffic control signal, it shall be used in addition to a Signal Ahead sign and shall be placed downstream from the Signal Ahead sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> When a Warning Beacon is interconnected with a traffic control signal or queue detection system, the BE PREPARED TO STOP sign should be supplemented with a WHEN FLASHING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">10a</span> <span class="ca">WHEN FLASHING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a>) plaque shall not be used to supplement the BE PREPARED TO STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-4.svg">W3-4</a>) sign. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">10b</span> <span class="ca">Studies indicate that the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a> plaque is generally not effective as a warning device for motorists approaching signalized </span> <span class="ca">intersections. Not using the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a> plaque also addresses the situation when a warning beacon is inoperative for any reason.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c45">Section 2C.45</a> contains information regarding the use of a NO MERGE AREA (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-5ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-5aP.svg">W4-5aP</a>) supplemental plaque in conjunction with a Yield Ahead sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> <span class="ca">An oversized square shape version of <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-1.svg">W1-1</a> should be used if installed on the outside curve in line with and at approximately a </span> <span class="ca">right angle to exiting traffic from a short ramp length with limiting curve near ramp terminal (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-3">Figure 2C-3</a>) where <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-1.svg">W3-1</a> and </span> <span class="ca"><a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-3.svg">W3-3</a> signs have proven ineffective. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> <span class="ca">A SIGNAL AHEAD (SW26aP(CA)) plaque or a STOP AHEAD (SW26bP(CA)) plaque (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-8ca">Figure 2C-8(CA)</a>) shall be </span> <span class="ca">used below an oversized square shape version of <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-1.svg">W1-1</a> at short ramp lengths with limiting curve near ramp terminals to </span> <span class="ca">indicate that a signal or stop sign configuration is present at the terminal. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-1.svg">W3-1</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-3.svg">W3-3</a> signs should be left in place when the oversized <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-1.svg">W1-1</a> sign with its accompanying plaque is placed. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c36" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.36 DRAW BRIDGE Sign (W3-6)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.36 &middot; manual page 327 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.36/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A DRAW BRIDGE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-6.svg">W3-6</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-8">Figure 2C-8</a>) shall be used in advance of movable bridge signals and gates (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4q/#4q02">Section 4Q.02</a>) to give warning to road users.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> <span class="ca">Where physical conditions prevent a motorist driving at the legal speed limit from having a continuous view of at least one signal </span> <span class="ca">indication before reaching the stop line, an auxiliary device should be provided in advance of movable bridge signals and gates. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> <span class="ca">This device may be either a supplemental signal or the mandatory DRAW BRIDGE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-6.svg">W3-6</a>) sign to which has been added a </span> <span class="ca">flashing yellow beacon interconnected with movable bridge control. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c37" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.37 Advance Ramp Control Signal Signs (W3-7 and W3-8)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.37 &middot; manual page 327 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.37/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">00</span> <span class="ca">For State highways, refer to Caltrans’ Ramp Metering Design Manual. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a05">Section 1A.05</a> for information regarding this </span> <span class="ca">publication. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A RAMP METER AHEAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-7.svg">W3-7</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-8">Figure 2C-8</a>) may be used to warn road users that a freeway entrance ramp is metered and that they will encounter a ramp control signal (see Chapter 4P).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> When the ramp control signals are <span class="ca">in operation</span> operated only during certain periods of the day, a RAMP METERED WHEN FLASHING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-8.svg">W3-8</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-8">Figure 2C-8</a>) <span class="ca">or an overhead Activated Blank-Out “METER ON” message (W88-2(CA) </span> <span class="ca">and W88-3(CA)) sign, or “PREPARE TO STOP” message (W89(CA)) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-8ca">Figure 2C-8(CA)</a>)</span> should be installed in advance of the ramp control signal near the entrance to the ramp, or on the arterial on the approach to the ramp, to alert road users to the presence and operation of ramp meters. <span class="ca">.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The RAMP METERED WHEN FLASHING sign shall be supplemented with a Warning Beacon (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4s/#4s03">Section 4S.03</a>) that flashes when the ramp control signal is in operation.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c38" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.38 NEW TRAFFIC PATTERN and NEW SIGNAL OPERATION AHEAD Signs (W23-2 and W23-2a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.38 &middot; manual page 327 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.38/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A NEW TRAFFIC PATTERN AHEAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w23-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W23-2.svg">W23-2</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-8">Figure 2C-8</a>) may be used on the approach to an intersection or along a section of roadway to provide advance warning of a change in traffic patterns, such as revised lane usage or roadway geometry.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A NEW SIGNAL OPERATION AHEAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w23-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W23-2a.svg">W23-2a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-8">Figure 2C-8</a>) may be used on the approach to a signalized intersection to provide advance warning of a change in signal phasing.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The NEW TRAFFIC PATTERN or NEW SIGNAL OPERATION AHEAD sign should be removed when the traffic pattern returns to normal, when the changed pattern is no longer considered to be new, or within 12 months.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c39" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.39 WATCH FOR STOPPED TRAFFIC Sign (W26-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.39 &middot; manual page 327 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.39/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The WATCH FOR STOPPED TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w26-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W26-1.svg">W26-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-8">Figure 2C-8</a>) may be used to warn road users of the possibility of vehicles stopping abruptly in the travel lane due to recurring congested conditions.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> <span class="ca">The WATCH FOR STOPPED TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w26-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W26-1.svg">W26-1</a>) sign may be used to alert road users in areas with limited sight distance where </span> <span class="ca">they could encounter stopped traffic unexpectedly. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> <span class="ca">A Warning Beacon (refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4s/#4s03">Section 4S.03</a>) or yellow LEDs within the border of the sign may be used with the WATCH FOR </span> <span class="ca">STOPPED TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w26-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W26-1.svg">W26-1</a>) sign. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c40" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.40 Reduced Speed Limit Ahead and Speed Zone Signs (W3-5, W3-5a, W3-5b, and W3-5c)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.40 &middot; manual page 328 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.40/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Reduced Speed Limit Ahead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-5.svg">W3-5</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-5a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-5a.svg">W3-5a</a>) or Truck Speed Zone Ahead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-5c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-5c.svg">W3-5c</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-9">Figure 2C-9</a>) should be used to inform road users of a reduced speed zone where the speed limit is being reduced by more than 10 mph, or where engineering judgment indicates the need for advance notice to comply with the posted speed limit ahead.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A VARIABLE SPEED ZONE AHEAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-5b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-5b.svg">W3-5b</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-9">Figure 2C-9</a>) should be used to inform road users of a zone where the speed limit is varied by time of day or as conditions change.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If used, Reduced Speed Limit, Variable Speed Zone, or Truck Speed Zone Ahead signs shall be followed by a Speed Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-1.svg">R2-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-3">Figure 2B-3</a>), with the Trucks (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-2p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-2P.svg">R2-2P</a> <span class="ca">M4-4P</span>) plaque <span class="ca">or the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-3.svg">R6-3</a>(CA) and R6-4(CA) signs</span> (see Figure 2B- 3<span class="ca">(CA)</span>) <span class="ca">on separate installations (refer to chapter 2B)</span> if applicable, installed at the beginning of the zone where the speed limit applies.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The speed limit displayed on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-5.svg">W3-5</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-5a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-5a.svg">W3-5a</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-5c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-5c.svg">W3-5c</a> signs shall be identical to the speed limit displayed on the subsequent Speed Limit sign.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c41" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.41 Intersection Warning Signs (W2-1 through W2-8)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.41 &middot; manual page 328 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.41/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Cross Road (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-1.svg">W2-1</a>), Side Road (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-2.svg">W2-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-3.svg">W2-3</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-3a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-3a.svg">W2-3a</a>), T-Intersection (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-4.svg">W2-4</a>), or Y-Intersection (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-5.svg">W2-5</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-10">Figure 2C-10</a>) may be used in advance of an intersection to indicate the presence of an intersection and the possibility of turning or entering traffic.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The Circular Intersection (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-6.svg">W2-6</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-10">Figure 2C-10</a>) may be installed in advance of a circular intersection (see Figures 2B-21 through 2B-23).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If an approach to a circular intersection has a statutory or posted speed limit of 40 mph or higher, the Circular Intersection (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-6.svg">W2-6</a>) sign should be installed in advance of the circular intersection.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> An educational plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-10">Figure 2C-10</a>) with a legend such as TRAFFIC CIRCLE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-12p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-12P.svg">W16-12P</a>) or ROUNDABOUT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-12ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-12aP.svg">W16-12aP</a>) may be mounted below a Circular Intersection sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c65">Section 2C.65</a> contains information about the use of an advance street name plaque to identify an intersecting road.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The Intersection Warning sign should illustrate and depict the general configuration of the intersecting roadway, such as a cross road, side road, T-intersection, or Y-intersection.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Intersection Warning signs, other than the Circular Intersection (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-6.svg">W2-6</a>) sign, the T-intersection (W2- 4) sign, and the Grade Crossing and Intersection Advance Warning (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w10-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W10-2.svg">W10-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w10-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W10-3.svg">W10-3</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w10-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W10-4.svg">W10-4</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w10-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W10-11.svg">W10-11</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w10-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W10-12.svg">W10-12</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-8b-4">Figure 8B-4</a>) should not be used on approaches controlled by STOP signs, YIELD signs, or signals.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> If an Intersection Warning sign is used where the side roads are not opposite of each other, the Offset Side Roads (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-7.svg">W2-7</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-10">Figure 2C-10</a>) should be used instead of the Cross Road sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> If an Intersection Warning sign is used where two closely-spaced side roads are on the same side of the highway, the Double Side Roads (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-8.svg">W2-8</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-10">Figure 2C-10</a>) should be used instead of the Side Road sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> No more than two side roads should be depicted on the same side of the highway on a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-7.svg">W2-7</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-8.svg">W2-8</a> sign, and no more than three side roads should be depicted on a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-7.svg">W2-7</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-8.svg">W2-8</a> sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> When at least one side road is shown, the stem of an additional side road representing a significantly lower relative volume may be depicted using a line that is two-thirds the width of the through road based on engineering judgment.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> <a href="#fig-2a-4">Figure 2A-4</a> shows examples of the typical placement of an Intersection Warning sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> <span class="ca">The Cross Traffic Ahead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w70ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w70ca.webp">W70(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-10ca">Figure 2C-10(CA)</a>) should be used to warn motorists that crossing at grade </span> <span class="ca">can be expected at non-signalized intersections in an expressway corridor. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> <span class="ca">If two freeway sections are connected by a short expressway segment or if multiple non-signalized intersections are close </span> <span class="ca">together, the Next Distance (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3aP.svg">W7-3aP</a>) plaque may be placed below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w70ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w70ca.webp">W70(CA)</a> sign. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c24">Section 2C.24</a> for DEAD END (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-1.svg">W14-1</a>) sign. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c42" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.42 Actuated Advance Intersection Signs (W2-10 and W2-11)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.42 &middot; manual page 329 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.42/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Actuated Advance Intersection signs are typically associated with restricted sight distance and gap selection at stop controlled intersections.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The TRAFFIC ENTERING WHEN FLASHING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-10.svg">W2-10</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-10">Figure 2C-10</a>) may be used on the uncontrolled through roadway approach to a side or cross road stop controlled intersection to warn of entering traffic from the side or cross road.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The TRAFFIC APPROACHING WHEN FLASHING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-11.svg">W2-11</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-10">Figure 2C-10</a>) may be used on the side road stop controlled approach to warn of traffic approaching on the uncontrolled through road.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> When used, the TRAFFIC ENTERING WHEN FLASHING sign, and the TRAFFIC APPROACHING WHEN FLASHING sign shall be supplemented with a Warning Beacon (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4s/#4s03">Section 4S.03</a>) that activates when a vehicle on a conflicting approach is detected.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <span class="ca">WHEN FLASHING is not used in California to address the situation when a warning beacon is inoperative for any reason. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c43" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.43 Two-Direction Large Arrow Sign (W1-7)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.43 &middot; manual page 329 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.43/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Two-Direction Large Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-7.svg">W1-7</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-10">Figure 2C-10</a>) shall be a horizontal rectangle.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the Two-Direction Large arrow sign shall be installed on the far side of a T-intersection in line with, and at approximately a right angle to, traffic approaching from the stem of the T- intersection.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The Two-Direction Large Arrow sign shall not be used where there is no change in the direction of travel such as at the beginnings and ends of medians or at center piers.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The Two-Direction Large Arrow sign should be visible for a sufficient distance to provide the road user with adequate time to react to the intersection configuration.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om1-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM1-3.svg">OM1-3</a> object marker should be used below and on the same post as the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-7.svg">W1-7</a> sign. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c70">Section 2C.70</a>. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c44" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.44 Traffic Signal Oncoming Extended Green Signs (W25-1 and W25-2)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.44 &middot; manual page 329 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.44/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> At locations where either a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w25-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W25-1.svg">W25-1</a> or a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w25-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W25-2.svg">W25-2</a> sign is required based on the provisions in <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4f/#4f01">Section 4F.01</a>, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w25-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W25-1.svg">W25-1</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w25-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W25-2.svg">W25-2</a> sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-10">Figure 2C-10</a>) shall be installed near the left-most signal face for the approach.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> <span class="ca">The “yellow trap” should be eliminated rather than trying to correct it with these signs. Refer to Part 4. </span></p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Merging, Two-Way Traffic, And No Passing Warning Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2c45" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.45 Merge Signs and Plaque (W4-1, W4-5, and W4-5aP)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.45 &middot; manual page 330 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.45/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Merge (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-1.svg">W4-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) may be used to warn road users on the major roadway that merging movements might be encountered in advance of a point where lanes from two separate roadways converge as a single traffic lane and no turning conflict occurs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">When installed at freeway entrance ramps, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-1.svg">W4-1</a> sign should be installed in advance of the paved gore area. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A Merge sign may also be installed on the side of the entering roadway to warn road users on the entering roadway of the merge condition.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The Merge sign should be installed on the side of the major roadway where merging traffic will be encountered and in such a position as to not obstruct the road user’s view of entering traffic.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> When a Merge sign is installed on a major roadway, the symbol should be oriented right or left as appropriate to depict the side from which the merge occurs, with the arrow representing the major roadway and the curved stem representing the entering roadway (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> When a Merge sign is to be installed on an entering roadway that curves before merging with the major roadway, such as a ramp with a curving horizontal alignment as it approaches the major roadway, the Entering Roadway Merge (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-5.svg">W4-5</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) should be used to better portray the actual geometric conditions to road users on the entering roadway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Where two roadways of approximately equal importance converge and merging movements are required, a Merge sign should be placed on each roadway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The Merge sign should not be used where two roadways converge and merging movements are not required.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The Merge sign shall not be used in place of a Lane Ends (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-2.svg">W4-2</a>) sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c47">Section 2C.47</a>) where lanes of traffic moving on a single roadway must merge because of a reduction in the actual or usable pavement width.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> An Entering Roadway Merge (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-5.svg">W4-5</a>) sign with a NO MERGE AREA (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-5ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-5aP.svg">W4-5aP</a>) supplemental plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) mounted below it may be used to warn road users on an entering roadway that they will encounter an abrupt merging situation without an acceleration lane at the downstream end of the ramp.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> A Merge (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-1.svg">W4-1</a>) sign with a NO MERGE AREA (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-5ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-5aP.svg">W4-5aP</a>) supplemental plaque mounted below it may be used to warn road users on the major roadway that traffic on an entering roadway will encounter an abrupt merging situation without an acceleration lane at the downstream end of the ramp.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> For a yield-controlled channelized right-turn movement onto a roadway without an acceleration lane, a NO MERGE AREA (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-5ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-5aP.svg">W4-5aP</a>) supplemental plaque may be mounted below a Yield Ahead (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W3-2.svg">W3-2</a>) sign and/or below a YIELD (R1- 2) sign when engineering judgment indicates that road users would expect an acceleration lane to be present.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> Examples of the use of Merge (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-1.svg">W4-1</a>) signs are shown in Drawing A in <a href="#fig-2c-12">Figure 2C-12</a>.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c46" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.46 Added Lane Signs (W4-3 and W4-6)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.46 &middot; manual page 330 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.46/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Added Lane (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-3.svg">W4-3</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) should be installed in advance of a point where two roadways converge and merging movements are not required. When possible, the Added Lane sign should be placed such that it is visible from both roadways; if this is not possible, an Added Lane sign should be placed on the side of each roadway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">When installed at freeway entrance ramps, the sign should be installed in advance of the paved gore area. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> When an Added Lane (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-3.svg">W4-3</a>) sign is installed on a major roadway, the symbol should be oriented right or left as appropriate to depict the side from which the entering roadway converges, with the straight arrow representing the major roadway and the curved arrow representing the entering roadway. The sign should be located on the side of the major roadway from which the entering roadway converges.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> When an Added Lane sign is to be installed on a roadway that curves before converging with another roadway that has a tangent alignment at the point of convergence, the Entering Roadway Added Lane (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-6.svg">W4-6</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) should be used to better portray the actual geometric conditions to road users on the curving roadway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Examples of the use of Added Lane (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-3.svg">W4-3</a>) and Entering Roadway Added Lane (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-6.svg">W4-6</a>) signs are shown in Drawing B in <a href="#fig-2c-12">Figure 2C-12</a>.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c47" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.47 Lane Ends Signs (W4-2 and W9-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.47 &middot; manual page 331 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.47/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Lane Ends (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-2.svg">W4-2</a>) and RIGHT (LEFT) LANE ENDS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-1.svg">W9-1</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) are used to warn of the reduction in the number of traffic lanes in the direction of travel.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The sequence of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-2.svg">W4-2</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-1.svg">W9-1</a> signs is illustrated in <a href="#fig-2c-13">Figure 2C-13</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The Lane Ends (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-2.svg">W4-2</a>) sign should be installed at the advance placement distance in accordance with <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> A RIGHT (LEFT) LANE ENDS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-1.svg">W9-1</a>) sign may <span class="ca">should</span>be installed in advance of the Lane Ends sign to provide additional warning that a lane is ending and that a merging maneuver will be required.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> If a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-1.svg">W9-1</a> sign is installed, a Distance (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-2p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-2P.svg">W16-2P</a> series or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-3p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-3P.svg">W16-3P</a> series) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) should be installed below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-1.svg">W9-1</a> sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> On one-way streets or on divided highways where the left-hand lane is ending and the width of the median will permit, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-1.svg">W9-1</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-2.svg">W4-2</a> signs should be placed facing approaching traffic on the left-hand side or median.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Where a lane ends a distance beyond the intersection that is less than the advance placement distance indicated in <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a>, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-2.svg">W4-2</a> sign may be located at the far side of the intersection (see Sheet 4 of <a href="#fig-2c-13">Figure 2C-13</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> When the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-2.svg">W4-2</a> sign is located at the far side of the intersection in accordance with Paragraph 7 of this Section, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-1.svg">W9-1</a> sign should be placed upstream of the intersection with the appropriate distance plaque.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3b/#3b12">Section 3B.12</a> contains information regarding the use of pavement markings in conjunction with a lane reduction.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> Lane Ends signs should not be installed in advance of the downstream end of an acceleration lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-2.svg">W4-2</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-1.svg">W9-1</a> signs shall not be used in dropped lane situations. In dropped lane situations on conventional roads at intersections, regulatory signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b28">Section 2B.28</a>) shall be used to inform road users that a through lane becomes a mandatory turn lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w74ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w74ca.webp">W74(CA)</a> sign should not be used for a lane reduction. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c48" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.48 Lanes Merge Signs (W9-4 and W4-8)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.48 &middot; manual page 331 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.48/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The LANES MERGE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-4.svg">W9-4</a>) and Single-Lane Transition (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-8.svg">W4-8</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) are used to warn of a merge of two lanes to one in the same direction of travel with a merging maneuver required for each lane (see Sheet 5 of <a href="#fig-2c-13">Figure 2C-13</a>). This type of merge is for a geometric condition where both approach lanes merge into a single lane, not where one lane merges into the other. <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/6h/#6h08">Section 6H.08</a> contains information about the use of the late merge sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The Single-Lane Transition (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-8.svg">W4-8</a>) sign should be located at the advance placement distance in accordance with <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The Lanes Merge (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-4.svg">W9-4</a>) sign may be used in advance of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-8.svg">W4-8</a> sign to provide additional warning that both lanes form a single lane and that a merging maneuver is needed for the traffic in each lane.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c49" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.49 HEAVY MERGE FROM LEFT (RIGHT) Sign (W4-7)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.49 &middot; manual page 332 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.49/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
