<p><span class="pn">01</span> The HEAVY MERGE FROM LEFT (RIGHT) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-7.svg">W4-7</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) may be used to supplement a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-1.svg">W4-1</a> sign at multilane approaches to congested areas to inform road users that it is desirable for through traffic to move out of a lane that will be occupied by a high volume of entering traffic. If used, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-7.svg">W4-7</a> sign may be supplemented with a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-2p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-2P.svg">W16-2P</a> series or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-3p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-3P.svg">W16-3P</a> series plaque (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c61">Section 2C.61</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-7.svg">W4-7</a> sign shall be installed at a location upstream from the location of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-1.svg">W4-1</a> sign.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c50" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.50 RIGHT (LEFT) LANE FOR EXIT ONLY Sign (W9-7)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.50 &middot; manual page 332 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.50/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">00</span> <span class="ca">The THRU TRAFFIC MERGE LEFT (RIGHT) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w74ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w74ca.webp">W74(CA)</a>) sign shall be used on freeways and expressways to inform </span> <span class="ca">motorists that the outside or inside lane is being dropped at the next exit, and through traffic must merge into the adjacent </span> <span class="ca">lane. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The RIGHT (LEFT) LANE FOR EXIT ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-7.svg">W9-7</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) may be used to provide advance warning to road users that traffic in the right-hand (left-hand) lane of a roadway will be required to depart the roadway at the next exit.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">The RIGHT (LEFT) LANE FOR EXIT ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-7.svg">W9-7</a>) sign shall be placed between the THRU TRAFFIC MERGE LEFT </span> <span class="ca">(RIGHT) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w74ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w74ca.webp">W74(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-11ca">Figure 2C-11(CA)</a>) and the RIGHT (LEFT) LANE MUST EXIT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a>) sign, at locations where </span> <span class="ca">overhead Exit Only signs (E11-1 Series) are not in place for lane drops at freeway exit ramps. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01b</span> <span class="ca">A RIGHT (LEFT) X LANES FOR EXIT ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w73bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w73bca.webp">W73B(CA)</a>) sign may be used in lieu of a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-7.svg">W9-7</a> if more than one lane will be </span> <span class="ca">required to depart the roadway at the next exit.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-7.svg">W9-7</a> sign should be installed upstream from the first overhead guide sign that contains an EXIT ONLY sign panel or upstream from the first RIGHT (LEFT) LANE MUST EXIT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a>) regulatory sign, if used, whichever is farther upstream from the exit.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> A legend or plaque displaying the distance may be added to the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w9-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W9-7.svg">W9-7</a> sign where the distance along the dropped lane between the sign and the exit ramp is greater than 1 mile.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b31">Section 2B.31</a> contains information regarding a regulatory sign that can be used for lane drops at grade-separated interchanges.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <span class="ca">On expressways, the THRU TRAFFIC MERGE LEFT (RIGHT) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w74ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w74ca.webp">W74(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-11ca">Figure 2C-11(CA)</a>) may be used in </span> <span class="ca">advance of the RIGHT(LEFT) LANE MUST TURN RIGHT(LEFT) sign (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a>). </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> <span class="ca">On conventional highways, the RIGHT(LEFT) LANE TURNS RIGHT(LEFT) AHEAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w73aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w73aca.webp">W73A(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-8ca">Figure 2C-8(CA)</a>) </span> <span class="ca">should be used in advance of the RIGHT(LEFT) LANE MUST TURN RIGHT(LEFT) sign (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a>). </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> <span class="ca">A</span><span class="ca">THRU TRAFFIC MERGE LEFT (RIGHT) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w74ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w74ca.webp">W74(CA)</a>) sign may be placed in advance of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w73aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w73aca.webp">W73A(CA)</a> sign. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="#fig-3b-11ca">Figure 3B-11(CA)</a> for lane drop markings at exit ramps. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c51" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.51 Two-Way Traffic Sign (W6-3)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.51 &middot; manual page 332 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.51/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Two-Way Traffic (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-3.svg">W6-3</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) should be used to warn road users of a transition from a multi- lane divided section of roadway to a two-lane, two-way section of roadway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A Two-Way Traffic (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-3.svg">W6-3</a>) sign with an AHEAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-9p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-9P.svg">W16-9P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) should be used to warn road users of a transition from a one-way street to a two-lane, two-way section of roadway (see <a href="#fig-2b-18">Figure 2B-18</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The Two-Way Traffic sign may be used at intervals along a two-lane, two-way roadway and may be used to supplement the Divided Highway (Road) Ends (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-2.svg">W6-2</a>) sign discussed in <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c21">Section 2C.21</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/6h/#6h17">Section 6H.17</a> contains information on a Narrow Two-Way Traffic (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-4.svg">W6-4</a>) sign for use in temporary traffic control situations.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <span class="ca">The Two-Way Traffic (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-3.svg">W6-3</a>) sign should also be used at locations where motorists could perceive that they are on a one-way </span> <span class="ca">roadway when, in fact, they are on a two lane, two-way highway. Following are some typical situations: </span> <span class="ca">A.</span><span class="ca">Construction sites where a two-lane highway is being converted to a freeway or an expressway. </span> <span class="ca">B.</span><span class="ca">Two-lane, two-way highways where ultimate freeway or expressway right-of-way has been purchased and grading for the </span> <span class="ca">full width has been completed. </span> <span class="ca">C.</span><span class="ca">Two-lane, two-way highways following long sections of multi-lane freeway or expressway. </span> <span class="ca">D.</span><span class="ca">Two-way highway with edge lines but with no centerlines. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> <span class="ca">The TWO WAY TRAFFIC (W44aP(CA)) plaque (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-11ca">Figure 2C-11(CA)</a>), if used, shall be positioned below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-3.svg">W6-3</a>, </span> <span class="ca"><a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-5.svg">W6-5</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-5a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-5a.svg">W6-5a</a> signs. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> <span class="ca">The Black on Yellow PASS WITH CARE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w83pca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w83pca.webp">W83P(CA)</a>) plaque (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-11ca">Figure 2C-11(CA)</a>), when used, shall be positioned </span> <span class="ca">below the Two Way Traffic (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-3.svg">W6-3</a>) sign where two-way traffic is being routed over a single roadway of a divided highway </span> <span class="ca">and passing is permitted. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="#fig-3b-14ca">Figure 3B-14(CA)</a> for signing and marking applications for lane reductions. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> <span class="ca">Typical example of <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-3.svg">W6-3</a> sign application is shown in <a href="#fig-3b-103ca">Figure 3B-103(CA)</a>. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c52" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.52 Two-Way Traffic on a Three-Lane Roadway Signs (W6-5 and W6-5a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.52 &middot; manual page 333 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.52/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Two-Way Traffic on a Three-Lane Roadway (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-5.svg">W6-5</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w6-5a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W6-5a.svg">W6-5a</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) may be installed along three-lane roadways with two lanes in one direction and one in the opposing direction.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c53" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.53 NO PASSING ZONE Sign (W14-3)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.53 &middot; manual page 333 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.53/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The NO PASSING ZONE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-3.svg">W14-3</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-11">Figure 2C-11</a>) shall be a pennant-shaped isosceles triangle with its longer axis horizontal and pointing to the right. When used, the NO PASSING ZONE sign shall be installed on the left-hand side of the roadway at the beginning of no-passing zones identified by pavement markings or DO NOT PASS signs or both (see Sections 2B.36 and 3B.03).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> <span class="ca">The NO PASSING ZONE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-3.svg">W14-3</a>) sign may be used at the beginning of no-passing zones identified by either pavement </span> <span class="ca">markings or DO NOT PASS signs or both (refer to Sections 2B.36 and 3B.03). </span></p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Miscellaneous Warning Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2c54" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.54 Vehicular Traffic Warning Signs (W8-6, W11-1, W11-5, W11-8, W11-10, W11- 11, W11- 12P, W11-14, W11-15, and W11-15a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.54 &middot; manual page 334 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.54/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Vehicular Traffic Warning (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-6.svg">W8-6</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-1.svg">W11-1</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-5.svg">W11-5</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-8.svg">W11-8</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-10.svg">W11-10</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-11.svg">W11-11</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-12p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-12P.svg">W11-12P</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-14/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-14.svg">W11-14</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15.svg">W11-15</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15a.svg">W11-15a</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-14">Figure 2C-14</a>) may be used to alert road users to locations where unexpected entries into the roadway by trucks, bicycles, farm vehicles, emergency vehicles, golf carts, horse- drawn vehicles, or other vehicles might occur. The TRUCK CROSSING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w8-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W8-6.svg">W8-6</a>) word message sign may be used as an alternate to the Truck (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-10.svg">W11-10</a>) symbol sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> These locations might be relatively confined or might occur randomly over a segment of roadway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Vehicular Traffic Warning signs should be used only at locations where the road user’s sight distance is restricted, or the condition, activity, or entering traffic would be unexpected.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If the condition or activity is seasonal or temporary, the Vehicular Traffic Warning sign should be removed or covered when the condition or activity does not exist.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The Trail Crossing (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15.svg">W11-15</a>) sign may be used where both bicyclists and pedestrians might be crossing the roadway, such as at an intersection with a shared-use path. A TRAIL X-ING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15P.svg">W11-15P</a>) supplemental plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-14">Figure 2C-14</a>) may be mounted below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15.svg">W11-15</a> sign. The TRAIL CROSSING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15a.svg">W11-15a</a>) sign may be used to warn of shared-use path crossings where pedestrians, bicyclists, and other user groups might be crossing the roadway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-1.svg">W11-1</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15.svg">W11-15</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15a.svg">W11-15a</a> signs and their related supplemental plaques may have a fluorescent yellow- green background with a black legend and border.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Supplemental plaques (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a> and <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c57">Section 2C.57</a>) with legends such as AHEAD, XX FEET, NEXT XX MILES, IN STREET, or IN ROAD may be mounted below Vehicular Traffic Warning signs to provide advance notice to road users of unexpected entries.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">07a</span> <span class="ca">On state highways, the IN STREET plaque should not be used. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">07b</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c67">Section 2C.67</a> for more information for IN STREET and IN ROAD plaques. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> If used in advance of a trail crossing, a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15.svg">W11-15</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15a.svg">W11-15a</a> sign should be supplemented with an AHEAD or XX FEET plaque to inform road users that they are approaching a point where crossing activity might occur.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> If a post-mounted <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-1.svg">W11-1</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-11.svg">W11-11</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15.svg">W11-15</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15a.svg">W11-15a</a> sign is placed at the location of the crossing point where golf carts, pedestrians, bicyclists, or other shared-use path users might be crossing the roadway, a diagonal downward-pointing arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-7p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-7P.svg">W16-7P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a> and <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c63">Section 2C.63</a>) shall be mounted below the sign. If the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-1.svg">W11-1</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-11.svg">W11-11</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15.svg">W11-15</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15a.svg">W11-15a</a> sign is mounted overhead, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-7p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-7P.svg">W16-7P</a> supplemental plaque shall not be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> A Vehicular Traffic Warning sign assembly shall not be installed on an approach controlled by a STOP or a YIELD sign, except as provided in Paragraphs 11 and 12 of this Section.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> The Vehicular Traffic Warning sign assembly may be installed on an approach to a circular intersection controlled by a YIELD sign where the crosswalk is at least 20 feet in advance of the yield point at the entrance to the circulatory roadway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> At a signalized or stop-controlled intersection the Vehicular Traffic Warning sign assembly may be installed on an approach to a channelized right-turn lane controlled by a YIELD sign where the crosswalk is at least 20 feet in advance of the yield point.</p>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> The crossing location identified by a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-1.svg">W11-1</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-11.svg">W11-11</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15.svg">W11-15</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-15a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-15a.svg">W11-15a</a> sign may be defined with crosswalk markings (see Chapter 3C).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> The Emergency Vehicle (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-8.svg">W11-8</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-14">Figure 2C-14</a>) with the EMERGENCY SIGNAL AHEAD (W11- 12P) supplemental plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-14">Figure 2C-14</a>) shall be placed in advance of all emergency-vehicle traffic control signals (see Chapter 4M).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> The Emergency Vehicle (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-8.svg">W11-8</a>) sign, or a word message sign indicating the type of emergency vehicle (such as rescue squad), may be used in advance of the emergency-vehicle station when no emergency- vehicle traffic control signal is present.</p>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> A Warning Beacon (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4s/#4s03">Section 4S.03</a>) may be used with any Vehicular Traffic Warning sign to indicate specific periods when the condition or activity is present or is likely to be present, or to provide enhanced sign conspicuity.</p>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> A supplemental WHEN FLASHING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) may be used with any Vehicular Traffic Warning sign that is supplemented with a Warning Beacon to indicate specific periods when the condition or activity is present or is likely to be present.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> <span class="ca">WHEN FLASHING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a>) plaque shall not be used to supplement any Vehicular Traffic Warning sign. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">19</span> <span class="ca">Studies indicate that the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a> plaque is generally not effective as a warning device for motorists approaching signalized </span> <span class="ca">intersections. Not using the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a> plaque also addresses the situation when a warning beacon is inoperative for any reason. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">20</span> <span class="ca">The Golf Cart (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-11.svg">W11-11</a>) signs may be used to alert road users to locations where unexpected entries into the roadway by golf </span> <span class="ca">carts might occur, such as at golf cart crossings. Refer to CVC § 38025. Also refer to CVC § 21115.1. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">21</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-11.svg">W11-11</a> sign may also be used in combination with the IN ROAD plaque sign at locations where a local agency permits the </span> <span class="ca">sharing of the roadway with slower moving golf carts. Refer to CVC § 21115. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">22</span> <span class="ca">The ALL TERRAIN VEHICLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw47aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw47aca.webp">SW47A(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-14ca">Figure 2C-14(CA)</a>) may be used in advance of a segment of highway </span> <span class="ca">that permits the use of regular vehicular traffic and also the driving of off highway motor vehicles on that portion of the highway. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">23</span> <span class="ca">A Next Distance (W7-3a) plaque should supplement the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw47aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw47aca.webp">SW47A(CA)</a>. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">24</span> <span class="ca">The WATCH FOR SNOW REMOVAL EQUIPMENT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw58ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw58ca.webp">SW58(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-14ca">Figure 2C-14(CA)</a>) may be used on highways </span> <span class="ca">leading to snow areas. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">25</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw58ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw58ca.webp">SW58(CA)</a> sign should be covered or removed during the summer season. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">26</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw58ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw58ca.webp">SW58(CA)</a> sign is normally placed at lower elevations where the first snow is usually encountered. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c55" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.55 Non-Vehicular Warning Signs (W11-2, W11-3, W11-4, W11-6, W11-7, W11-9, and W11-16 through W11-22)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.55 &middot; manual page 335 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.55/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Non-Vehicular Warning (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-2.svg">W11-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-3.svg">W11-3</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-4.svg">W11-4</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-6.svg">W11-6</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-7.svg">W11-7</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-9.svg">W11-9</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-16/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-16.svg">W11-16</a> through W11- 22 <span class="ca">and </span> <span class="ca"><a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-23ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w11-23ca.webp">W11-23(CA)</a></span>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2c-15">Figure 2C-15</a> <span class="ca">and 2C-15(CA)</span>) may be used to alert road users in advance of locations where unexpected entries into the roadway might occur or where shared use of the roadway by pedestrians, animals, or equestrians might occur.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to CVC §§ 21364 and 21365 for the Cattle (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-4.svg">W11-4</a>) sign. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">01b</span> <span class="ca">Refer to CVC § 21805 for the Equestrian (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-7.svg">W11-7</a>) sign. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01c</span> <span class="ca">The Deer Crossing (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-3.svg">W11-3</a>) sign should be used only after confirmation from a Department of Fish and Wildlife warden having </span> <span class="ca">jurisdiction in the area that a substantial problem exists. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01d</span> <span class="ca">The Bear (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-16/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-16.svg">W11-16</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-15">Figure 2C-15</a>) may be used in advance of an area known to be inhabited by bear and there </span> <span class="ca">have been reported instances where bears are crossing the roadway. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01e</span> <span class="ca">The WILDLIFE CROSSING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-23ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w11-23ca.webp">W11-23(CA)</a>) sign should be used to alert road users of multiple types of wild animals present in or </span> <span class="ca">by the sides of the roadways. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-23ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w11-23ca.webp">W11-23(CA)</a> sign should not be used in lieu of the individual symbol signs for animals. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01f</span> <span class="ca">The WILDLIFE CROSSING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-23ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/w11-23ca.webp">W11-23(CA)</a>) sign may be used to alert road users of a wild animal which may cause traffic safety </span> <span class="ca">hazard and for which there are currently no symbol signs available. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01g</span> <span class="ca">Since wildlife migration patterns can change over time, it is recommended that the placement of non-vehicular warning signs </span> <span class="ca">featuring wildlife be periodically reviewed to verify that they continue to reflect current crossing behavior and remain effective. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01h</span> <span class="ca">The DEAF CHILDREN NEAR (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw38ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw38ca.webp">SW38(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-15ca">Figure 2C-15(CA)</a>) may be used on city streets or county roads to </span> <span class="ca">indicate that a deaf child is near. Refer to CVC § 21351.7. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">01i</span> <span class="ca">The SENIOR ZONE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw50-1pca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw50-1pca.webp">SW50-1P(CA)</a>) plaque or SENIOR (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw50-2pca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw50-2pca.webp">SW50-2P(CA)</a>) plaque (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-15ca">Figure 2C-15(CA)</a>)</span><span class="ca">may be used in </span> <span class="ca">combination, above the Speed Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-1.svg">R2-1</a> (25,20 or 15)) sign on any street or road, other than a State highway, with a speed limit </span> <span class="ca">greater than 25 mph that is adjacent to a senior citizen facility. Refer to CVC §§ 22352 and 22358.4. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01j</span> <span class="ca">If used, the NEXT XX MILES supplemental plaque should be placed at approximately 5 mile intervals, or when intersecting </span> <span class="ca">major traffic generators.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> These conflicts might be relatively confined, or might occur randomly over a segment of roadway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If used in advance of a pedestrian, snowmobile, or equestrian crossing, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-2.svg">W11-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-6.svg">W11-6</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-7.svg">W11-7</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-9.svg">W11-9</a> signs should be supplemented with plaques (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a> and <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c61">Section 2C.61</a>) with the legend AHEAD or XX FEET to inform road users that they are approaching a point where crossing activity might occur.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If a post-mounted <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-2.svg">W11-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-6.svg">W11-6</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-7.svg">W11-7</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-9.svg">W11-9</a> sign is placed at the location of the crossing point where pedestrians, snowmobilers, or equestrians might be crossing the roadway, a diagonal downward-pointing arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-7p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-7P.svg">W16-7P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a> and <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c63">Section 2C.63</a>) shall be mounted below the sign. If the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-2.svg">W11-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-6.svg">W11-6</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-7.svg">W11-7</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-9.svg">W11-9</a> sign is mounted overhead, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-7p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-7P.svg">W16-7P</a> plaque shall not be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> A Non-Vehicular Warning sign assembly shall not be installed on an approach controlled by a STOP or a YIELD sign, except as provided in Paragraphs 6 and 7 of this Section.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The Non-Vehicular Warning sign assembly may be installed on an approach to a circular intersection controlled by a YIELD sign where the crosswalk is at least 20 feet in advance of the yield point at the entrance to a circulatory roadway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> At a signalized or stop-controlled intersection the Non-Vehicular Warning sign assembly may be installed on an approach to a channelized right-turn lane controlled by a YIELD sign where the crosswalk is at least 20 feet in advance of the yield point.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> A Pedestrian Crossing (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-2.svg">W11-2</a>) sign may be placed overhead or may be post-mounted with a diagonal downward- pointing arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-7p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-7P.svg">W16-7P</a>) plaque at the crosswalk location where Yield Here To (Stop Here For) Pedestrians signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b19">Section 2B.19</a>) have been installed in advance of the crosswalk.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> If a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-2.svg">W11-2</a> sign has been post-mounted at the crosswalk location where a Yield Here To (Stop Here For) Pedestrians sign is used on the approach, the Yield Here To (Stop Here For) Pedestrians sign shall not be placed on the same post as the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-2.svg">W11-2</a> sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> An advance Pedestrian Crossing (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-2.svg">W11-2</a>) sign with an AHEAD or a distance supplemental plaque may be used in conjunction with a Yield Here To (Stop Here For) Pedestrians sign on the approach to the same crosswalk.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> The crossing location identified by a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-2.svg">W11-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-6.svg">W11-6</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-7.svg">W11-7</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-9.svg">W11-9</a> sign may be defined with crosswalk markings (see Chapter 3C).</p>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-2.svg">W11-2</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w11-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W11-9.svg">W11-9</a> signs and their related supplemental plaques may have a fluorescent yellow- green background with a black legend and border.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> When a fluorescent yellow-green background is used, a systematic approach featuring one background color within a zone or area should be used. The mixing of standard yellow and fluorescent yellow-green backgrounds within a selected site area should be avoided.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> A Warning Beacon (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4s/#4s03">Section 4S.03</a>) may be used with any Non-Vehicular Warning sign to indicate specific periods when the condition or activity is present or is likely to be present, or to provide enhanced sign conspicuity.</p>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> A supplemental WHEN FLASHING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) may be used with any Non-Vehicular Warning sign that is supplemented with a Warning Beacon to indicate specific periods when the condition or activity is present or is likely to be present.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> <span class="ca">WHEN FLASHING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a>) plaque shall not be used to supplement any Non-Vehicular Warning sign. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> <span class="ca">Studies indicate that the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a> plaque is generally not effective as a warning device for motorists approaching signalized </span> <span class="ca">intersections. Not using the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-13p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-13P.svg">W16-13P</a> plaque also addresses the situation when a warning beacon is inoperative for any reason. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c56" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.56 Playground Sign (W15-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.56 &middot; manual page 337 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.56/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Playground (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w15-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W15-1.svg">W15-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2c-15">Figure 2C-15</a>) may be used to give advance warning of a designated children’s playground that is located adjacent to the road.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The Playground sign may have a fluorescent yellow-green background with a black legend and border.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If the access to the playground area requires a roadway crossing, the application of crosswalk pavement markings (see Chapter 3C) and Non-Vehicular Warning signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c55">Section 2C.55</a>) should be considered.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> <span class="ca">The PLAYGROUND (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw49pca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw49pca.webp">SW49P(CA)</a>) plaque (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-15ca">Figure 2C-15(CA)</a>) should not be used alone. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sw49pca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sw49pca.webp">SW49P(CA)</a> plaque may be used in combination above the Speed Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-1.svg">R2-1</a> (25)) sign and WHEN CHILDREN ARE </span> <span class="ca">PRESENT (S4-2) sign on any street or road, other than a State highway, with a speed limit greater than 25 mph that is adjacent to a </span> <span class="ca">children’s playground within a public park. Refer to CVC § 22357.1. </span></p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Supplemental Warning Plaques</div>


<span id="2c57" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.57 Use of Supplemental Warning Plaques

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.57 &middot; manual page 338 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.57/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A supplemental warning plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) may be displayed with a warning or regulatory sign when engineering judgment indicates that road users require additional warning information beyond that contained in the main message of the warning or regulatory sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Supplemental warning plaques shall be used only in combination with and installed on the same post(s) as warning or regulatory signs. They shall not be mounted alone or displayed alone.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Unless otherwise provided in this Manual for a particular plaque, supplemental warning plaques shall be mounted below the sign they supplement.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c58" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.58 Design of Supplemental Warning Plaques

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.58 &middot; manual page 338 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.58/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A supplemental warning plaque used with a warning sign shall have the same legend, border, and background color as the warning sign with which it is displayed. A supplemental warning plaque used with a regulatory sign shall have a black legend and border on a yellow background.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Supplemental warning plaques shall be square or rectangular.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c59" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.59 Advisory Speed Plaque (W13-1P) and Confirmation Advisory Speed Plaque (W13-1aP)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.59 &middot; manual page 338 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.59/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Advisory Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1P.svg">W13-1P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) may be used to supplement an advance warning sign to indicate the advisory speed for a condition.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The Confirmation Advisory Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1aP.svg">W13-1aP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-1">Figure 2C-1</a>) may be used to supplement a One-Direction Large Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-6.svg">W1-6</a>) sign on the outside of a turn or curve in line with and at approximately a right angle to approaching traffic.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The use of the Advisory Speed and Confirmation Advisory Speed plaques for horizontal curves shall be in accordance with <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c06">Section 2C.06</a> and <a href="#fig-2c-6">Table 2C-6</a>. The speed differential in <a href="#fig-2c-6">Table 2C-6</a> shall be the difference between the advisory speed for the horizontal curve and the posted speed limit, statutory speed limit, or the 85th percentile speed on the approach to the curve. The Advisory Speed plaque shall also be used where an engineering study indicates a need to advise road users of the advisory speed for other roadway conditions.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The speed displayed on the Advisory Speed and Confirmation Advisory Speed plaques shall be a multiple of 5 mph.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Except in emergencies or when the condition is temporary, an Advisory Speed or Confirmation Advisory Speed plaque shall not be installed until the advisory speed has been determined by an engineering study.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The Advisory Speed plaque shall only be used to supplement an advance warning sign. The Advisory Speed plaque or the Confirmation Advisory Speed plaque shall not be installed as a separate sign installation.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The Advisory Speed plaque, if used with a sign that is also supplemented with another plaque, such as an Advance Street Name plaque (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c65">Section 2C.65</a>), should be mounted immediately below the primary warning sign with any other plaque mounted below the Advisory Speed plaque.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The Confirmation Advisory Speed plaque shall only be used to supplement a One-Direction Large Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w1-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W1-6.svg">W1-6</a>) sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c10">Section 2C.10</a>) or an Exit Gore (E5-1 series) sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2e/#2e26">Section 2E.26</a>) and shall not be installed as a separate sign installation.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The advisory speed shall be determined by an engineering study that follows established engineering practices.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> The advisory speed should be determined based on free-flowing traffic conditions.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> Because changes in conditions, such as roadway geometrics, surface characteristics, or sight distance, might affect the advisory speed, each location should be evaluated periodically or when conditions change.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> Among the established engineering practices that are appropriate for the determination of the recommended advisory speed for a horizontal curve are the following: A.Compass method B.Safety-based method C.Accelerometer method D.Design equation method E.Ball-bank method using the following criteria: 1.16 degrees of ball-bank for speeds of 20 mph or less 2.14 degrees of ball-bank for speeds of 25 to 30 mph 3.12 degrees of ball-bank for speeds of 35 mph and higher</p>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> The 16, 14, and 12 degrees of ball-bank criteria are comparable to the current AASHTO horizontal curve design guidance. Research has shown that drivers often exceed existing posted advisory curve speeds by 7 to 10 mph.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> <span class="ca">If used, the speed shown on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1P.svg">W13-1P</a> plaque shall not be in excess of the posted or maximum speed limit. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> <span class="ca">The Advisory Speed plaque shall not be used in conjunction with any sign other than a warning sign, nor shall it be </span> <span class="ca">used alone. When used, it shall be positioned below the warning sign. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> <span class="ca">In determining the need for curve or turn warning signs, consideration should be given to driver expectancy based on the driving </span> <span class="ca">environment. If the curve can be driven at legal speed without discomfort, there is normally no need for a sign. A curve warning sign </span> <span class="ca">should be considered in advance of any curve that produces a reading of 10 degrees on a Ball Bank Indicator at speeds lower than </span> <span class="ca">the approach speed. If a curve warning sign is needed, it should be supplemented with an advisory speed message. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> <span class="ca">A mechanical or electronic Ball Indicator should be used to determine the advisory speed for curves.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> <span class="ca">This speed is shown on Advisory Exit and Ramp Speed Signs and Advisory Speed Plaques. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">19</span> <span class="ca">The Advisory Speed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w13-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W13-1P.svg">W13-1P</a>) plaque may also be used with a number of other warning signs. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">20</span> <span class="ca">One method of determining the advisory speed is to drive the curve at several selected uniform speeds and plot the Ball Bank </span> <span class="ca">Indicator readings as shown in <a href="#fig-2c-101ca">Figure 2C-101(CA)</a>. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">21</span> <span class="ca">A minimum of three speed runs should be made in each direction. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c60" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.60 NEW Plaque (W16-15P)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.60 &middot; manual page 339 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.60/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p>01 A NEW (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-15p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-15P.svg">W16-15P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) may be mounted above a regulatory sign when a new regulation takes effect in order to alert road users to the new traffic regulation. A NEW plaque may also be mounted above an advance warning sign (such as a Signal Ahead sign for a newly-installed traffic control signal) for a warning of a new traffic condition.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p>02 The NEW plaque should be removed no later than 6 <span class="ca">12</span> months after it was installed.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> <span class="ca">Refer to FHWA’s List of Known Errors for error in Paragraph 2 text. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a04">Section 1A.04</a> for more details. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c61" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.61 Distance Plaques (W16-2 Series, W16-3 Series, W16-4P, and W7-3aP)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.61 &middot; manual page 339 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.61/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Distance Ahead (W16-2 series and W16-3 series) plaques (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) may be used to inform the road user of the distance to the condition indicated by the warning sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The Next Distance (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3aP.svg">W7-3aP</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-4p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-4P.svg">W16-4P</a>) plaques (see Figures 2C-5 and 2C-16) may be used to inform road users of the length of roadway over which the condition indicated by the warning sign exists.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> <span class="ca">When the distance is in miles, the mileage shown should be to the nearest 1/4 mile for a distance of less than 1 mile and to the </span> <span class="ca">nearest mile for distances over one mile. The text “MILE” should be used for a distance of one mile or less. The text “MILES” should </span> <span class="ca">be used for distances over one mile. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c62" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.62 Supplemental Arrow Plaques (W16-5P and W16-6P)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.62 &middot; manual page 340 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.62/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> If the condition indicated by a warning sign is located on an intersecting road and the distance between the intersection and condition is not sufficient to provide adequate advance placement of the warning sign, a Supplemental Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-5p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-5P.svg">W16-5P</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-6p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-6P.svg">W16-6P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) should be used below the warning sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Supplemental Arrow plaques shall have the same legend design as the Advance Turn Arrow and Directional Arrow auxiliary signs (see Sections 2D.26 and 2D.28) except that they shall have a black legend and border on a yellow or fluorescent <span class="ca">yellow or</span> yellow-green background, as appropriate.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c63" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.63 Diagonal Downward-Pointing Arrow Plaques (W16-7P and W16-7aP)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.63 &middot; manual page 340 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.63/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Diagonal downward-pointing arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-7p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-7P.svg">W16-7P</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-7ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-7aP.svg">W16-7aP</a>) plaques (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) are used with certain Vehicular Traffic Warning signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c54">Section 2C.54</a>) and certain Non-Vehicular Warning signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c55">Section 2C.55</a>), and School Crossing signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/7b/#7b03">Section 7B.03</a>) to indicate the specific location of a crossing point.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-7p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-7P.svg">W16-7P</a> plaque contains a single arrow pointing diagonally down to the right or left, toward the roadway, depending on which side of the roadway it is located.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> A <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-7ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-7aP.svg">W16-7aP</a> plaque may be used with a single crossing sign located on a narrow median separating two roadways with traffic in the same direction where the crossing traverses both roadways.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> <span class="ca">The Diagonal Downward-Pointing Arrow Plaque <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-7p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-7P.svg">W16-7P</a> may be used where object markers (refer to Sections 2C.70 and </span> <span class="ca">2C.72) may be ineffective, with the downward arrow either left or right, to mark obstructions in the roadway where traffic is permitted </span> <span class="ca">to pass on one side only. </span></p>
</div>

<span id="2c64" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.64 Hill-Related Plaques (W7-2 Series and W7-3 Series)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.64 &middot; manual page 340 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.64/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Hill-Related (W7-2 series and W7-3 series) plaques (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a>) or other appropriate legends and larger signs should be used for emphasis or where special hill characteristics exist.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> On longer grades, the use of a distance (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3aP.svg">W7-3aP</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w7-3bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W7-3bP.svg">W7-3bP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-5">Figure 2C-5</a>) at periodic intervals of approximately 1-mile spacing should be considered.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c65" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.65 Advance Street Name Plaques (W16-8P and W16-8aP)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.65 &middot; manual page 340 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.65/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> An Advance Street Name (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-8p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-8P.svg">W16-8P</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-8ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-8aP.svg">W16-8aP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) may be used with any Intersection (W1- 10 series, W2 series, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w10-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W10-2.svg">W10-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w10-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W10-3.svg">W10-3</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w10-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W10-4.svg">W10-4</a>) or Advance Traffic Control (W3 series) sign to identify the name of the intersecting street.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The lettering on Advance Street Name plaques shall be composed of a combination of lower- case letters with initial upper-case letters.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If two street names are used on the Advance Street Name plaque, a directional arrow pointing in the direction of the street shall be placed next to each street name. Arrows pointing to the left shall be placed to the left of the street name, and arrows pointing to the right shall be placed to the right of the street name.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If two street names are used on the Advance Street Name plaque, the street names and associated arrows should <span class="ca">shall</span>be displayed in the following order: A. For a single intersection, the name of the street to the left should <span class="ca">shall</span>be displayed above the name of the street to the right; or B. For two sequential intersections, such as where the plaque is used with an Offset Side Roads (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-7.svg">W2-7</a>) or a Double Side Road (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w2-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W2-8.svg">W2-8</a>) sign, the name of the first street encountered should <span class="ca">shall</span>be displayed above the name of the second street encountered, and the arrow associated with the second street encountered should shall be an advance arrow, such as the arrow shown on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-6p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-6P.svg">W16-6P</a> arrow plaque (see Figure 2C- 16).</p>
</div>

<span id="2c66" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.66 Traffic Does Not Stop Plaques (W4-4P Series)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.66 &middot; manual page 341 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.66/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The CROSS TRAFFIC DOES NOT STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-4p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-4P.svg">W4-4P</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-10">Figure 2C-10</a>) may be used in combination with a STOP sign when engineering judgment indicates that conditions are present that are causing or could cause road users to misinterpret the intersection as an all-way stop.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">The CROSS TRAFFIC DOES NOT STOP (W4-4p) plaque should be used in combination with a STOP sign at two-way stop-</span> <span class="ca">controlled intersections when a conversion from four-way stop to two-way stop operation is implemented.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The TRAFFIC FROM LEFT (RIGHT) DOES NOT STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-4ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-4aP.svg">W4-4aP</a>) or ONCOMING TRAFFIC DOES NOT STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-4bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-4bP.svg">W4-4bP</a>) plaque may be used when such messages more accurately describe the traffic controls established at the intersection.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-4ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-4aP.svg">W4-4aP</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-4bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-4bP.svg">W4-4bP</a> plaques should be used at intersections where STOP signs control all but one approach to the intersection, unless the only non-stopped approach is from a one-way street.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w4-4p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W4-4P.svg">W4-4P</a> series plaque is used, it shall be mounted below the STOP sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/9c/#9c06">Section 9C.06</a> contains information for Bicycle Cross Traffic warning plaques that can be used below STOP signs on crossroads or driveways that intersect with bicycle facilities.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c67" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.67 IN ROAD and IN STREET Plaques (W16-1P and W16-1aP)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.67 &middot; manual page 341 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.67/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> In situations where there is a need to warn drivers to watch for other slower forms of transportation traveling along the highway, such as bicycles, pedestrians, golf carts, horse-drawn vehicles, or farm machinery, an IN ROAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-1P.svg">W16-1P</a>) plaque or IN STREET (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-1ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-1aP.svg">W16-1aP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2c-16">Figure 2C-16</a>) may be used.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">On state highways, the IN STREET plaque should not be used. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The background color of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-1P.svg">W16-1P</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-1ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-1aP.svg">W16-1aP</a> plaque shall match the background color of the warning sign with which it is displayed. If a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-1p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-1P.svg">W16-1P</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-1ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-1aP.svg">W16-1aP</a> plaque is used, it shall be mounted below either a Vehicular Traffic Warning sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c54">Section 2C.54</a>) or a Non-Vehicular Warning sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c55">Section 2C.55</a>), and shall not be mounted alone.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/9b/#9b14">Section 9B.14</a> contains information about the use of a Bicycles Allowed Use of Full Lane (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-20/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-20.svg">R9-20</a>) sign to inform drivers of the presence of bicycles in the roadway or where bicyclists are expected or preferred to use the full lane.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c68" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.68 EXCEPT BICYCLES Plaque (W16-20P)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.68 &middot; manual page 341 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.68/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Where it is desired to notify bicyclists that the conditions depicted by a warning sign are not applicable to bicycles, the EXCEPT BICYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-20p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-20P.svg">W16-20P</a>) supplemental warning plaque (see Figure 2C- 16) may be mounted below the warning sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Examples of warning signs with which an EXCEPT BICYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-20p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-20P.svg">W16-20P</a>) plaque can be mounted include DEAD END (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-1.svg">W14-1</a>) or NO OUTLET (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-2.svg">W14-2</a>) signs.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c69" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.69 Photo Enforced Plaques (W16-10P and W16-10aP)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.69 &middot; manual page 342 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.69/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Photo Enforced (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-10p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-10P.svg">W16-10P</a>) plaque or a PHOTO ENFORCED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w16-10ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W16-10aP.svg">W16-10aP</a>) word message plaque (see Figure 2C- 16) may be mounted below a warning sign to advise road users that the regulations associated with the condition being warned about (such as a traffic control signal or a toll plaza) are being enforced by photographic equipment.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Object Markers</div>


<span id="2c70" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.70 Object Marker Design and Placement Height

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.70 &middot; manual page 343 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.70/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Types 1, 2, and 3 object markers are used to mark obstructions within or adjacent to the roadway. Type 4 object markers are used to mark the end of a roadway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> When used, object markers (see <a href="#fig-2c-17">Figure 2C-17</a> <span class="ca">and 2C-17(CA)</span>) shall not have a border and shall consist of an arrangement of one or more of the following types: Type 1—a diamond-shaped sign, at least 18 inches on a side, consisting of either a yellow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om1-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM1-1.svg">OM1-1</a>) or black (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om1-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM1-2.svg">OM1-2</a>) sign with nine yellow retroreflective devices, each with a minimum diameter of 3 inches, mounted symmetrically on the sign, or an all-yellow retroreflective sign (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om1-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM1-3.svg">OM1-3</a>). Type 2—either a marker (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om2-1v/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM2-1V.svg">OM2-1V</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om2-1h/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM2-1H.svg">OM2-1H</a>) consisting of three yellow retroreflective devices, each with a minimum diameter of 3 inches, arranged either horizontally or vertically on a white sign measuring at least 6 x 12 inches; or an all-yellow horizontal or vertical retroreflective sign (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om2-2v/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM2-2V.svg">OM2-2V</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om2-2h/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM2-2H.svg">OM2-2H</a>), measuring at least 6 x 12 inches. Type 3—a striped marker, 12 x 36 inches, consisting of a vertical rectangle with alternating black and retroreflective yellow stripes sloping downward at an angle of 45 degrees toward the side of the obstruction on which traffic is to pass. The minimum width of the yellow and black stripes shall be 3 inches. Type 4—a diamond-shaped sign, at least 18 inches on a side, consisting of either a red (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM4-1.svg">OM4-1</a>) or black (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM4-2.svg">OM4-2</a>) sign with nine red retroreflective devices, each with a minimum diameter of 3 inches, mounted symmetrically on the sign, or an all-red retroreflective sign (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/om4-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/OM4-3.svg">OM4-3</a>). <span class="ca">Type L(CA) Utility Pole marker (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-17ca">Figure 2C-17(CA)</a>) shall be yellow retroreflective material consisting of three 2 x </span> <span class="ca">12 inch horizontal rectangles arranged vertically on a utility pole. </span> <span class="ca">Type Q(CA) object marker (refer to <a href="#fig-2c-17ca">Figure 2C-17(CA)</a>) shall be a vertical tubular marker, with a height of 18 to 24 inch </span> <span class="ca">and a minimum cross sectional dimension of 2 ¼ inch. The yellow retroreflective material shall consist of three bands, </span> <span class="ca">each 3 inch in height or a single band 9 inch in height. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Type 3 object markers with stripes that begin at the upper right side and slope downward to the lower left side are designated as right object markers (OM3-R). Object markers with stripes that begin at the upper left side and slope downward to the lower right side are designated as left object markers (OM3-L). Object markers with chevron stripes that slope downward to both the lower left and lower right sides are designated as center object markers (OM3-C).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> When used for marking obstructions within the roadway or obstructions that are 8 feet or less from the shoulder or curb, the minimum mounting height, measured from the bottom of the object marker to the elevation of the near edge of the traveled way, should be 4 feet.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> When used to mark obstructions more than 8 feet from the shoulder or curb, the clearance from the ground to the bottom of the object marker should be at least 4 feet.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Object markers should not present a vertical or horizontal clearance obstacle for pedestrians.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> When object markers or markings are applied to an obstruction that by its nature requires a lower or higher mounting, the vertical mounting height may vary according to need.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/9c/#9c09">Section 9C.09</a> contains information regarding the use of object markers on shared-use paths.</p>
</div>

<span id="2c71" class="anchor"></span>

## 2C.71 Object Markers for Obstructions Within the Roadway

<div class="secmeta">Section 2C.71 &middot; manual page 343 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2C.71/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Obstructions within the roadway shall be marked with a Type 1 or Type 3 object marker. In addition to markers on the face of the obstruction, warning of approach to the obstruction shall be given by appropriate pavement markings (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3b/#3b13">Section 3B.13</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">The Type Q(CA) object marker may be used to emphasize objects within the roadway, for example, curb noses, where it is </span> <span class="ca">desirable that the marker be visible from all directions. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> To provide additional emphasis, a Type 1 or Type 3 object marker may be installed at or near the approach end of a median island.</p>
